Experiments in challenging domains, such as spreadsheet, VisionQA and math reasoning, show that Trace2Skill significantly improves upon strong baselines, including Anthropic's official xlsx skills. Crucially, this trajectory-grounded evolution does not merely memorize task instances or model-specific quirks: evolved skills transfer across LLM scales and generalize to OOD settings. For example, skills evolved by Qwen3.5-35B on its own trajectories improved a Qwen3.5-122B agent by up to 57.65 absolute percentage points on WikiTableQuestions. Ultimately, our results demonstrate that complex agent experience can be packaged into highly transferable, declarative skills -- requiring no parameter updates, no external retrieval modules, and utilizing open-source models as small as 35B parameters.

</details>


### [95] [The Competence Shadow: Theory and Bounds of AI Assistance in Safety Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25197)
*Umair Siddique*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI助手在安全工程中的应用可能引入“能力阴影”，即系统性地缩小人类推理范围。本文提出形式化框架，证明AI辅助的效果取决于协作设计而非工具本身，需从工具认证转向工作流认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI助手被整合到物理AI系统的安全工程工作流中，一个关键问题浮现：AI辅助究竟是提高了安全分析质量，还是引入了仅在部署后事故中才会显现的系统性盲点？安全工程难以通过基准驱动进行评估，因为安全能力具有不可约的多维性，受限于上下文相关的正确性、固有的不完整性以及合理的专家分歧。

Method: 建立了安全分析中AI辅助的形式化框架。首先提出了五维能力框架，涵盖领域知识、标准专业知识、操作经验、情境理解和判断力。随后形式化定义了“能力阴影”概念，即AI生成的安全分析所导致的系统性人类推理收窄。形式化了四种典型的人机协作结构，并推导出闭式性能界限。

Result: 证明了能力阴影以乘法方式复合，导致的退化程度远超简单的加性估计。核心发现是：安全工程中的AI辅助是一个协作设计问题，而非软件采购决策。同一工具是降低还是提高分析质量，完全取决于其使用方式。推导出了抗阴影工作流的非退化条件。

Conclusion: AI辅助在安全工程中的应用效果完全取决于协作设计方式。研究呼吁从工具资格认证转向工作流资格认证，以实现可信的物理AI，确保AI助手真正提升而非损害安全分析质量。

Abstract: As AI assistants become integrated into safety engineering workflows for Physical AI systems, a critical question emerges: does AI assistance improve safety analysis quality, or introduce systematic blind spots that surface only through post-deployment incidents? This paper develops a formal framework for AI assistance in safety analysis. We first establish why safety engineering resists benchmark-driven evaluation: safety competence is irreducibly multidimensional, constrained by context-dependent correctness, inherent incompleteness, and legitimate expert disagreement. We formalize this through a five-dimensional competence framework capturing domain knowledge, standards expertise, operational experience, contextual understanding, and judgment.
  We introduce the competence shadow: the systematic narrowing of human reasoning induced by AI-generated safety analysis. The shadow is not what the AI presents, but what it prevents from being considered. We formalize four canonical human-AI collaboration structures and derive closed-form performance bounds, demonstrating that the competence shadow compounds multiplicatively to produce degradation far exceeding naive additive estimates.
  The central finding is that AI assistance in safety engineering is a collaboration design problem, not a software procurement decision. The same tool degrades or improves analysis quality depending entirely on how it is used. We derive non-degradation conditions for shadow-resistant workflows and call for a shift from tool qualification toward workflow qualification for trustworthy Physical AI.

</details>


### [96] [Probabilistic Abstract Interpretation on Neural Networks via Grids Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25266)
*Zhuofan Zhang,Herbert Wiklicky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将概率抽象解释理论应用于神经网络，提出了分析神经网络输入密度分布流的框架，并展示了其在实际问题中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当神经网络具有不可数或可数无穷多的输入时，测试所有输入变得不可行。为了解决这一问题，作者希望借鉴程序分析中的概率抽象解释理论，用于提取神经网络的性质。

Method: 将概率抽象解释理论迁移至神经网络，构建了相应的抽象域，定义了Moore-Penrose伪逆及抽象转换器，并在该框架下分析输入的密度分布流。

Result: 展示了该理论框架在神经网络中的可行性，给出了具体抽象域和对应的伪逆及转换器，并通过实验案例验证了框架在实际问题分析中的效果。

Conclusion: 概率抽象解释可有效应用于神经网络输入分析，为不可测输入空间下的网络性质提取提供了理论与实践基础。

Abstract: Probabilistic abstract interpretation is a theory used to extract particular properties of a computer program when it is infeasible to test every single inputs. In this paper we apply the theory on neural networks for the same purpose: to analyse density distribution flow of all possible inputs of a neural network when a network has uncountably many or countable but infinitely many inputs. We show how this theoretical framework works in neural networks and then discuss different abstract domains and corresponding Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverses together with abstract transformers used in the framework. We also present experimental examples to show how this framework helps to analyse real world problems.

</details>


### [97] [Distribution and Clusters Approximations as Abstract Domains in Probabilistic Abstract Interpretation to Neural Network Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25273)
*Zhuofan Zhang,Herbert Wiklicky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在概率抽象解释框架下，提出了分布近似和簇近似两种新颖的网格近似方法，用于分析神经网络的密度分布流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的概率抽象解释框架使用网格近似方法将具体空间抽象为网格，但该方法在分析神经网络输入的密度分布流时存在局限性，需要更有效的近似方法来改进分析的精度和效率。

Method: 提出了两种新的近似方法：分布近似和簇近似。论文详细阐述了这两种方法的理论基础，设计了相应的抽象转换器，并通过简单示例的图示说明其工作原理。

Result: 成功建立了分布近似和簇近似的理论框架，推导出了对应的抽象转换器，并通过图示验证了这两种方法在简单示例上的有效性。

Conclusion: 分布近似和簇近似为神经网络的概率抽象解释分析提供了新的抽象域选择，能够更好地捕捉输入空间的结构特征，为后续的实际应用和性能评估奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: The probabilistic abstract interpretation framework of neural network analysis analyzes a neural network by analyzing its density distribution flow of all possible inputs. The grids approximation is one of abstract domains the framework uses which abstracts concrete space into grids. In this paper, we introduce two novel approximation methods: distribution approximation and clusters approximation. We show how these two methods work in theory with corresponding abstract transformers with help of illustrations of some simple examples.

</details>


### [98] [A Gait Foundation Model Predicts Multi-System Health Phenotypes from 3D Skeletal Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25283)
*Adam Gabet,Sarah Kohn,Guy Lutsker,Shira Gelman,Anastasia Godneva,Gil Sasson,Arad Zulti,David Krongauz,Rotem Shaulitch,Assaf Rotem,Ohad Doron,Yuval Brodsky,Adina Weinberger,Eran Segal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于3,414名成年人的步态基础模型，证明了步态是一个独立的多系统生物信号，能够有效预测年龄、BMI、内脏脂肪等表型及临床诊断，有望成为一种可扩展的被动生命体征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前步态分析主要被视为特定病理的症状，而非系统性生物标志物。尽管步态日益被视为重要的生命体征，但缺乏将其作为独立多系统生物信号进行全面评估的方法，这限制了对步态临床价值的充分挖掘。

Method: 研究团队开发了基于3D骨骼运动的步态基础模型，使用深度相机记录3,414名深度表型分析成年人在五项运动任务中的数据。模型学习步态嵌入表示，并与传统工程特征进行对比。通过解剖学消融实验分析不同身体部位对不同表型预测的贡献，并调整年龄、BMI、内脏脂肪组织和身高等混杂因素。

Result: 学习到的嵌入表示在预测年龄(Pearson r=0.69)、BMI(r=0.90)和内脏脂肪组织面积(r=0.82)方面显著优于工程特征。嵌入表示能够显著预测3,210个表型目标中的1,980个；调整年龄、BMI、VAT和身高后，步态在男性18个身体系统和女性17个身体系统中均提供独立预测增益，并改善临床诊断和用药预测。解剖消融实验显示腿部主导代谢和衰弱预测，而躯干编码睡眠和生活方式表型。

Conclusion: 研究确立了步态作为独立多系统生物信号的地位，证明了其在预测多种生理表型和临床诊断方面的价值。这一发现推动了步态分析向消费者级视频的转化，为其作为可扩展、被动生命体征的整合应用奠定了基础，具有重要的临床转化意义。

Abstract: Gait is increasingly recognized as a vital sign, yet current approaches treat it as a symptom of specific pathologies rather than a systemic biomarker. We developed a gait foundation model for 3D skeletal motion from 3,414 deeply phenotyped adults, recorded via a depth camera during five motor tasks. Learned embeddings outperformed engineered features, predicting age (Pearson r = 0.69), BMI (r = 0.90), and visceral adipose tissue area (r = 0.82). Embeddings significantly predicted 1,980 of 3,210 phenotypic targets; after adjustment for age, BMI, VAT, and height, gait provided independent gains in all 18 body systems in males and 17 of 18 in females, and improved prediction of clinical diagnoses and medication use. Anatomical ablation revealed that legs dominated metabolic and frailty predictions while torso encoded sleep and lifestyle phenotypes. These findings establish gait as an independent multi-system biosignal, motivating translation to consumer-grade video and its integration as a scalable, passive vital sign.

</details>


### [99] [SliderQuant: Accurate Post-Training Quantization for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25284)
*Shigeng Wang,Chao Li,Yangyuxuan Kang,Jiawei Fan,Zhonghong Ou,Anbang Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SliderQuant，一种针对大语言模型的后训练量化框架。通过观察发现浅层和深层对量化更敏感，尤其是首尾层，因此采用自适应滑动窗口策略对不同层进行差异化量化设计，在多种LLM和任务上超越了现有PTQ方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PTQ方法对所有层采用统一的量化策略，但这在低比特设置下可能并非最优。作者通过实证研究发现：(1)浅层和深层比中间层对量化更敏感；(2)首尾层量化误差显著大于其他层。这些发现表明LLM的不同层需要多层次的差异化量化设计。

Method: 提出SliderQuant框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)层间滑动量化：针对浅层、中间层和深层不同的量化敏感性，设计三种新颖的滑动窗口策略；(2)层内滑动量化：利用增量策略对每个窗口进行量化。该框架通过少量可学习参数实现自适应滑动量化概念。

Result: 在Llama/Llama2/Llama3/Qwen2.5模型家族、DeepSeek-R1蒸馏模型和大型MoE模型上进行了广泛实验，涵盖基础语言生成、零样本常识推理、数学和代码任务。结果表明SliderQuant在仅权重量化和权重-激活量化两种场景下均优于现有PTQ方法（包括最新的旋转变换方法）。

Conclusion: SliderQuant通过针对不同层设计差异化的滑动窗口量化策略，有效降低了各层的量化误差。该方法证明了在LLM量化中考虑层间敏感度差异的重要性，为低比特量化提供了一种简单有效的解决方案，在保持模型性能的同时实现了更好的量化效果。

Abstract: In this paper, we address post-training quantization (PTQ) for large language models (LLMs) from an overlooked perspective: given a pre-trained high-precision LLM, the predominant sequential quantization framework treats different layers equally, but this may be not optimal in challenging bit-width settings. We empirically study the quantization impact of different layers on model accuracy, and observe that: (1) shallow/deep layers are usually more sensitive to quantization than intermediate layers; (2) among shallow/deep layers, the most sensitive one is the first/last layer, which exhibits significantly larger quantization error than others. These empirical observations imply that the quantization design for different layers of LLMs is required on multiple levels instead of a single level shared to all layers. Motivated by this, we propose a new PTQ framework termed Sliding-layer Quantization (SliderQuant) that relies on a simple adaptive sliding quantization concept facilitated by few learnable parameters. The base component of SliderQuant is called inter-layer sliding quantization, which incorporates three types of novel sliding window designs tailored for addressing the varying quantization sensitivity of shallow, intermediate and deep layers. The other component is called intra-layer sliding quantization that leverages an incremental strategy to quantize each window. As a result, SliderQuant has a strong ability to reduce quantization errors across layers. Extensive experiments on basic language generation, zero-shot commonsense reasoning and challenging math and code tasks with various LLMs, including Llama/Llama2/Llama3/Qwen2.5 model families, DeepSeek-R1 distilled models and large MoE models, show that our method outperforms existing PTQ methods (including the latest PTQ methods using rotation transformations) for both weight-only quantization and weight-activation quantization.

</details>


### [100] [DAGverse: Building Document-Grounded Semantic DAGs from Scientific Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25293)
*Shu Wan,Saketh Vishnubhatla,Iskander Kushbay,Tom Heffernan,Aaron Belikoff,Raha Moraffah,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DAGverse框架，利用科学论文中的显式DAG图作为监督信号，从文档中构建文档支撑的语义有向无环图（DAG），并发布了包含108个专家验证DAG的数据集DAGverse-1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的有向无环图（DAG）数据集稀缺，构建通常需要专家解读领域文档。该问题面临文档可能存在多种抽象、意图结构通常隐含、支持证据分散在文本、公式、标题和图表中等挑战。

Method: 利用包含显式DAG图的科学论文作为自然监督源（DAG图提供结构，文本提供上下文和解释），提出了DAGverse框架，其核心组件DAGverse-Pipeline是一个半自动系统，通过图分类、图重构、语义接地和验证四个步骤生成高精度语义DAG示例。

Result: 在因果DAG案例研究中发布了DAGverse-1数据集，包含108个专家验证的语义DAG，具备图级、节点级和边级证据。实验表明DAGverse-Pipeline在DAG分类和注释任务上优于现有视觉-语言模型。

Conclusion: DAGverse为文档支撑的DAG基准提供了基础，并为研究基于现实世界证据的结构化推理开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs) are widely used to represent structured knowledge in scientific and technical domains. However, datasets for real-world DAGs remain scarce because constructing them typically requires expert interpretation of domain documents. We study Doc2SemDAG construction: recovering a preferred semantic DAG from a document together with the cited evidence and context that explain it. This problem is challenging because a document may admit multiple plausible abstractions, the intended structure is often implicit, and the supporting evidence is scattered across prose, equations, captions, and figures. To address these challenges, we leverage scientific papers containing explicit DAG figures as a natural source of supervision. In this setting, the DAG figure provides the DAG structure, while the accompanying text provides context and explanation. We introduce DAGverse, a framework for constructing document-grounded semantic DAGs from online scientific papers. Its core component, DAGverse-Pipeline, is a semi-automatic system designed to produce high-precision semantic DAG examples through figure classification, graph reconstruction, semantic grounding, and validation. As a case study, we test the framework for causal DAGs and release DAGverse-1, a dataset of 108 expert-validated semantic DAGs with graph-level, node-level, and edge-level evidence. Experiments show that DAGverse-Pipeline outperforms existing Vision-Language Models on DAG classification and annotation. DAGverse provides a foundation for document-grounded DAG benchmarks and opens new directions for studying structured reasoning grounded in real-world evidence.

</details>


### [101] [Evaluating Language Models for Harmful Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25326)
*Canfer Akbulut,Rasmi Elasmar,Abhishek Roy,Anthony Payne,Priyanka Suresh,Lujain Ibrahim,Seliem El-Sayed,Charvi Rastogi,Ashyana Kachra,Will Hawkins,Kristian Lum,Laura Weidinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个评估AI有害操纵行为的框架，通过对10,101名参与者的跨领域、跨地区研究，发现AI模型在被提示时能产生操纵行为并改变用户信念与行为，且操纵效果因领域和地区而异，模型操纵倾向与成功率之间无一致预测关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对AI驱动有害操纵行为的评估方法存在局限性，缺乏针对特定情境的人类-AI交互研究框架，因此需要开发一种新的评估框架来系统性地研究AI操纵行为。

Method: 提出了基于特定情境的人类-AI交互研究框架，招募10,101名参与者，覆盖三个AI应用领域（公共政策、金融、健康）和三个地区（美国、英国、印度），通过实验评估AI模型的操纵行为及其效果。

Result: 发现测试的AI模型在被提示时能产生操纵行为并在实验环境中成功改变参与者的信念和行为；操纵行为在不同领域和地区间存在显著差异；模型产生操纵行为的频率（倾向性）与其操纵成功率（有效性）之间缺乏一致的预测关系。

Conclusion: AI操纵评估需要在其可能被使用的高风险情境中进行，且单一地理区域的研究结果可能无法推广到其他地区；研究公开了测试协议和材料以促进框架采纳，并讨论了AI模型有害操纵评估中的开放性挑战。

Abstract: Interest in the concept of AI-driven harmful manipulation is growing, yet current approaches to evaluating it are limited. This paper introduces a framework for evaluating harmful AI manipulation via context-specific human-AI interaction studies. We illustrate the utility of this framework by assessing an AI model with 10,101 participants spanning interactions in three AI use domains (public policy, finance, and health) and three locales (US, UK, and India). Overall, we find that that the tested model can produce manipulative behaviours when prompted to do so and, in experimental settings, is able to induce belief and behaviour changes in study participants. We further find that context matters: AI manipulation differs between domains, suggesting that it needs to be evaluated in the high-stakes context(s) in which an AI system is likely to be used. We also identify significant differences across our tested geographies, suggesting that AI manipulation results from one geographic region may not generalise to others. Finally, we find that the frequency of manipulative behaviours (propensity) of an AI model is not consistently predictive of the likelihood of manipulative success (efficacy), underscoring the importance of studying these dimensions separately. To facilitate adoption of our evaluation framework, we detail our testing protocols and make relevant materials publicly available. We conclude by discussing open challenges in evaluating harmful manipulation by AI models.

</details>


### [102] [Macroscopic Characteristics of Mixed Traffic Flow with Deep Reinforcement Learning Based Automated and Human-Driven Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25328)
*Pankaj Kumar,Pranamesh Chakraborty,Subrahmanya Swamy Peruru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于TD3算法的自动驾驶车辆控制方法，相比传统IDM模型，可将道路容量提升约7.52%，高速行驶时燃油效率提升约28.98%，且不牺牲安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通环境中，自动驾驶车辆需要在安全、效率、舒适性、燃油效率和交通规则合规性之间取得平衡，同时考虑异质性驾驶员行为。传统的跟驰模型（如IDM）难以在不同交通场景中泛化，且通常不考虑燃油效率，这促使研究者采用基于学习的方法。尽管深度强化学习在微观跟驰性能上表现出色，但其在宏观交通流特性方面的研究仍然不足。

Method: 采用Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3)算法实现自动驾驶车辆控制，使用NGSIM高速公路数据集进行训练，以实现与人类驾驶车辆的真实交互。通过基本图(Fundamental Diagram)在不同驾驶员异质性、异质时间间隙渗透率和不同强化学习控制车辆比例下评估交通性能，并对基于强化学习的自动驾驶模型与IDM进行宏观层面的燃油效率比较。

Result: 交通性能对安全时间间隙分布和强化学习车辆比例敏感。从完全人工驾驶过渡到完全强化学习控制交通，道路容量可增加约7.52%。强化学习自动驾驶车辆在高速（50 km/h以上）时平均燃油效率提升约28.98%，低速（50 km/h以下）时提升约1.86%。深度强化学习框架在不牺牲安全性的前提下提升了交通容量和燃油效率。

Conclusion: 基于深度强化学习的自动驾驶车辆控制方法能够有效改善混合交通环境中的宏观交通流特性和燃油效率，为未来自动驾驶车辆的部署提供了有力的理论支持和技术参考。

Abstract: Automated Vehicle (AV) control in mixed traffic, where AVs coexist with human-driven vehicles, poses significant challenges in balancing safety, efficiency, comfort, fuel efficiency, and compliance with traffic rules while capturing heterogeneous driver behavior. Traditional car-following models, such as the Intelligent Driver Model (IDM), often struggle to generalize across diverse traffic scenarios and typically do not account for fuel efficiency, motivating the use of learning-based approaches. Although Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has shown strong microscopic performance in car-following conditions, its macroscopic traffic flow characteristics remain underexplored. This study focuses on analyzing the macroscopic traffic flow characteristics and fuel efficiency of DRL-based models in mixed traffic. A Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) algorithm is implemented for AVs' control and trained using the NGSIM highway dataset, enabling realistic interaction with human-driven vehicles. Traffic performance is evaluated using the Fundamental Diagram (FD) under varying driver heterogeneity, heterogeneous time-gap penetration levels, and different shares of RL-controlled vehicles. A macroscopic level comparison of fuel efficiency between the RL-based AV model and the IDM is also conducted. Results show that traffic performance is sensitive to the distribution of safe time gaps and the proportion of RL vehicles. Transitioning from fully human-driven to fully RL-controlled traffic can increase road capacity by approximately 7.52%. Further, RL-based AVs also improve average fuel efficiency by about 28.98% at higher speeds (above 50 km/h), and by 1.86% at lower speeds (below 50 km/h) compared to the IDM. Overall, the DRL framework enhances traffic capacity and fuel efficiency without compromising safety.

</details>


### [103] [Agentic Trust Coordination for Federated Learning through Adaptive Thresholding and Autonomous Decision Making in Sustainable and Resilient Industrial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25334)
*Paul Shepherd,Tasos Dagiuklas,Bugra Alkan,Jonathan Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级智能体信任协调方法，通过在服务器端部署信任控制层来观察、推理并动态调整信任参数，从而在异构和资源受限的工业网络中实现更稳定可靠的联邦学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业网络中，联邦学习（FL）的可靠性受到客户端行为不一致、感知噪声以及恶意或故障更新的影响。现有的基于信任的缓解机制多为统计性和启发式方法，依赖固定参数或简单的自适应规则，难以适应动态变化的运行环境。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级智能体信任控制层（Agentic Trust Control Layer）。该框架作为服务器端控制环路运行，明确分离了观察、推理和行动三个过程：它监控信任相关和系统级信号，解释其随时间的演变，并在检测到不稳定时进行针对性的信任干预。该方法无需修改客户端训练过程或增加通信开销。

Result: 该框架通过实现上下文感知的干预决策，扩展了先前的自适应信任机制。它支持稳定的联邦学习操作，有效解决了固定或纯反应式参数更新难以应对动态工况的问题。

Conclusion: 所提出的智能体信任协调方法为可持续和弹性的工业网络提供了一种有效的联邦学习可靠性保障方案，在不增加额外通信负担的前提下，显著提升了系统对动态环境和异常行为的适应能力。

Abstract: Distributed intelligence in industrial networks increasingly integrates sensing, communication, and computation across heterogeneous and resource constrained devices. Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training in such environments, but its reliability is affected by inconsistent client behaviour, noisy sensing conditions, and the presence of faulty or adversarial updates. Trust based mechanisms are commonly used to mitigate these effects, yet most remain statistical and heuristic, relying on fixed parameters or simple adaptive rules that struggle to accommodate changing operating conditions.
  This paper presents a lightweight agentic trust coordination approach for FL in sustainable and resilient industrial networks. The proposed Agentic Trust Control Layer operates as a server side control loop that observes trust related and system level signals, interprets their evolution over time, and applies targeted trust adjustments when instability is detected. The approach extends prior adaptive trust mechanisms by enabling context aware intervention decisions, rather than relying on fixed or purely reactive parameter updates. By explicitly separating observation, reasoning, and action, the proposed framework supports stable FL operation without modifying client side training or increasing communication overhead.

</details>


### [104] [4OPS: Structural Difficulty Modeling in Integer Arithmetic Puzzles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25356)
*Yunus E. Zeytuncu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于动态规划的精确求解器，用于分析整数算术谜题的难度决定因素。通过构建340万个实例的数据集，发现难度完全由少数可解释的结构属性决定，特别是最小构造中使用的输入值数量，为自适应算术学习提供了可解释的难度估计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算术谜题游戏为研究数学推理任务中的难度提供了一个受控环境，这是自适应学习系统中的核心挑战。研究者希望理解这类谜题难度的结构性决定因素，以支持可解释的难度估计和合理的任务排序。

Method: 研究者首先形式化了整数算术谜题问题，开发了一个精确的动态规划求解器，该求解器能够枚举可达目标、提取最小操作见证，并支持大规模标注。利用此求解器构建了超过340万个实例的数据集，并通过到达目标所需的最小操作数来定义难度。

Result: 研究发现，基于包和目标级别统计的基线机器学习模型虽然能部分预测可解性，但无法可靠区分简单实例。相比之下，难度完全由少数从精确见证中导出的可解释结构属性决定，特别是在此标注下，最小构造中使用的输入值数量作为难度的最小充分统计量。

Conclusion: 这些结果提供了一个透明、基于计算的谜题难度解释，连接了符号推理和数据驱动建模。该框架支持可解释的难度估计和原则性任务排序，对自适应算术学习和智能练习系统具有直接意义。

Abstract: Arithmetic puzzle games provide a controlled setting for studying difficulty in mathematical reasoning tasks, a core challenge in adaptive learning systems. We investigate the structural determinants of difficulty in a class of integer arithmetic puzzles inspired by number games. We formalize the problem and develop an exact dynamic-programming solver that enumerates reachable targets, extracts minimal-operation witnesses, and enables large-scale labeling.
  Using this solver, we construct a dataset of over 3.4 million instances and define difficulty via the minimum number of operations required to reach a target. We analyze the relationship between difficulty and solver-derived features. While baseline machine learning models based on bag- and target-level statistics can partially predict solvability, they fail to reliably distinguish easy instances. In contrast, we show that difficulty is fully determined by a small set of interpretable structural attributes derived from exact witnesses. In particular, the number of input values used in a minimal construction serves as a minimal sufficient statistic for difficulty under this labeling.
  These results provide a transparent, computationally grounded account of puzzle difficulty that bridges symbolic reasoning and data-driven modeling. The framework supports explainable difficulty estimation and principled task sequencing, with direct implications for adaptive arithmetic learning and intelligent practice systems.

</details>


### [105] [Does Structured Intent Representation Generalize? A Cross-Language, Cross-Model Empirical Study of 5W3H Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25379)
*Peng Gang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将基于5W3H的结构化意图表示框架(PPS)扩展至英语和日语，发现AI辅助扩展的提示词在目标对齐上与人工编写的无显著差异，且能降低跨模型输出方差并纠正非结构化提示词的双重偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的结构化意图表示研究仅限于中文，本研究旨在探索该方法能否跨语言（英语、日语）和跨模型泛化，并评估AI辅助创作界面在降低用户门槛方面的有效性，同时探究跨模型输出一致性问题。

Method: 采用PPS（Prompt Protocol Specification）框架，基于5W3H维度进行结构化意图表示。实验设计了四种条件，其中包括AI自动将简单提示词扩展为完整5W3H规范的条件。在三种语言、三种大语言模型、60个任务上进行了2160次模型输出的对比实验。

Result: 1. AI扩展的5W3H提示词与人工编写的在目标对齐上无统计学显著差异，用户只需输入单句；2. 结构化PPS条件常降低或重塑跨模型输出方差，但在语言和指标间不一致；3. 非结构化提示词表现出系统性的双重膨胀偏差：复合分数虚高、跨模型方差虚低。

Conclusion: 结构化5W3H表示能提升跨语言、跨模型的意图对齐度和可访问性，AI辅助创作有效降低了非专家用户的使用门槛。但跨模型方差减少效果并非普适，需关注非结构化基线中的虚假低方差问题。

Abstract: Does structured intent representation generalize across languages and models? We study PPS (Prompt Protocol Specification), a 5W3H-based framework for structured intent representation in human-AI interaction, and extend prior Chinese-only evidence along three dimensions: two additional languages (English and Japanese), a fourth condition in which a user's simple prompt is automatically expanded into a full 5W3H specification by an AI-assisted authoring interface, and a new research question on cross-model output consistency. Across 2,160 model outputs (3 languages x 4 conditions x 3 LLMs x 60 tasks), we find that AI-expanded 5W3H prompts (Condition D) show no statistically significant difference in goal alignment from manually crafted 5W3H prompts (Condition C) across all three languages, while requiring only a single-sentence input from the user. Structured PPS conditions often reduce or reshape cross-model output variance, though this effect is not uniform across languages and metrics; the strongest evidence comes from identifying spurious low variance in unconstrained baselines. We also show that unstructured prompts exhibit a systematic dual-inflation bias: artificially high composite scores and artificially low apparent cross-model variance. These findings suggest that structured 5W3H representations can improve intent alignment and accessibility across languages and models, especially when AI-assisted authoring lowers the barrier for non-expert users.

</details>


### [106] [Beyond Content Safety: Real-Time Monitoring for Reasoning Vulnerabilities in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25412)
*Xunguang Wang,Yuguang Zhou,Qingyue Wang,Zongjie Li,Ruixuan Huang,Zhenlan Ji,Pingchuan Ma,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次提出“推理安全”概念，通过构建九类不安全推理行为分类法、大规模实证研究以及设计实时推理安全监控器，系统性地解决了大语言模型推理过程的安全问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型安全研究主要关注内容安全（如有害、偏见或事实错误的输出），而将推理链视为不透明的中间产物，忽略了推理过程本身的安全性。作者指出推理安全是一个正交且同等关键的安全维度，要求模型的推理轨迹具备逻辑一致性、计算效率性，并能抵抗对抗性操纵。

Method: 1. 形式化定义推理安全，并提出了包含九类不安全推理行为的分类法（涵盖输入解析错误、推理执行错误和过程管理错误）。\n2. 对4111条推理链进行大规模标注研究，来源包括自然推理基准和四种对抗攻击方法。\n3. 提出推理安全监控器（Reasoning Safety Monitor）：一个基于外部LLM的组件，通过嵌入分类法的提示词实时检查每个推理步骤，并在检测到不安全行为时发送中断信号。

Result: 1. 实证研究证实所有九种错误类型在实践中均存在，且每种攻击都会引发可机制解释的特征签名。\n2. 在450条链的静态基准测试中，监控器实现了高达84.88%的步骤级定位准确率和85.37%的错误类型分类准确率，显著优于幻觉检测器和过程奖励模型基线。

Conclusion: 推理级别的监控既是必要的也是实际可行的，研究结果确立了推理安全作为大型推理模型安全部署的基础性关注点，为未来研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning to solve complex tasks, yet the safety of the reasoning process itself remains largely unaddressed. Existing work on LLM safety focuses on content safety--detecting harmful, biased, or factually incorrect outputs -- and treats the reasoning chain as an opaque intermediate artifact. We identify reasoning safety as an orthogonal and equally critical security dimension: the requirement that a model's reasoning trajectory be logically consistent, computationally efficient, and resistant to adversarial manipulation. We make three contributions. First, we formally define reasoning safety and introduce a nine-category taxonomy of unsafe reasoning behaviors, covering input parsing errors, reasoning execution errors, and process management errors. Second, we conduct a large-scale prevalence study annotating 4111 reasoning chains from both natural reasoning benchmarks and four adversarial attack methods (reasoning hijacking and denial-of-service), confirming that all nine error types occur in practice and that each attack induces a mechanistically interpretable signature. Third, we propose a Reasoning Safety Monitor: an external LLM-based component that runs in parallel with the target model, inspects each reasoning step in real time via a taxonomy-embedded prompt, and dispatches an interrupt signal upon detecting unsafe behavior. Evaluation on a 450-chain static benchmark shows that our monitor achieves up to 84.88\% step-level localization accuracy and 85.37\% error-type classification accuracy, outperforming hallucination detectors and process reward model baselines by substantial margins. These results demonstrate that reasoning-level monitoring is both necessary and practically achievable, and establish reasoning safety as a foundational concern for the secure deployment of large reasoning models.

</details>


### [107] [Modernising Reinforcement Learning-Based Navigation for Embodied Semantic Scene Graph Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25415)
*Roman Kueble,Marco Hueller,Mrunmai Phatak,Rainer Lienhart,Joerg Haehner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于具身语义场景图生成的模块化导航组件，通过改进优化算法和动作表示，在有限动作预算下将场景图完整性提升了21%，并实现了完整性与效率的最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义世界模型能够支持具身智能体进行超越纯几何表示的推理，而语义场景图（SSG）为此提供了结构化和紧凑的表示方式。核心挑战在于如何在有限动作预算内获取最大化模型质量和下游实用性的观测，需要在信息增益与导航成本之间进行权衡，并判断何时额外行动会产生边际收益递减。

Method: 本文提出了一个模块化导航组件，主要改进包括：(1) 替换原有的策略优化方法；(2) 重新审视离散动作表述，研究了紧凑型和更细粒度的较大离散运动集；(3) 比较了原子动作的单头策略与动作组件的分解多头策略；(4) 评估了课程学习和可选的基于深度的碰撞监督。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1) 仅替换优化算法在相同奖励塑造下使SSG完整性相对基线提升21%；(2) 深度信息主要影响执行安全性（无碰撞运动），而对完整性影响不大；(3) 结合现代优化方法与更细粒度的分解动作表示，能够实现完整性与效率的最优权衡。

Conclusion: 现代优化技术与细粒度分解动作表示的结合是提升具身语义场景图生成性能的有效途径，能够在保证执行安全性的同时最大化场景图的完整性，为有限资源约束下的自主探索提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Semantic world models enable embodied agents to reason about objects, relations, and spatial context beyond purely geometric representations. In Organic Computing, such models are a key enabler for objective-driven self-adaptation under uncertainty and resource constraints. The core challenge is to acquire observations maximising model quality and downstream usefulness within a limited action budget.
  Semantic scene graphs (SSGs) provide a structured and compact representation for this purpose. However, constructing them within a finite action horizon requires exploration strategies that trade off information gain against navigation cost and decide when additional actions yield diminishing returns.
  This work presents a modular navigation component for Embodied Semantic Scene Graph Generation and modernises its decision-making by replacing the policy-optimisation method and revisiting the discrete action formulation. We study compact and finer-grained, larger discrete motion sets and compare a single-head policy over atomic actions with a factorised multi-head policy over action components. We evaluate curriculum learning and optional depth-based collision supervision, and assess SSG completeness, execution safety, and navigation behaviour.
  Results show that replacing the optimisation algorithm alone improves SSG completeness by 21\% relative to the baseline under identical reward shaping. Depth mainly affects execution safety (collision-free motion), while completeness remains largely unchanged. Combining modern optimisation with a finer-grained, factorised action representation yields the strongest overall completeness--efficiency trade-off.

</details>


### [108] [Cross-Model Disagreement as a Label-Free Correctness Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25450)
*Matt Gorbett,Suman Jana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出跨模型分歧作为无监督的正确性指示器，通过验证模型对生成模型回答的惊讶程度来判断错误，在多个基准上显著优于传统的不确定性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有依赖模型自身不确定性（如熵或置信度分数）的错误检测方法在面对最危险的失败模式——置信错误时失效，即模型错误但确信的情况。需要一种无需真实标签、能检测置信错误的新方法。

Method: 提出跨模型分歧原则，并实现为两种训练自由的方法：跨模型困惑度（CMP）测量验证模型对生成模型回答token的惊讶程度；跨模型熵（CME）测量验证模型在这些位置的确定性。仅需单次前向传播，无需验证模型生成或正确性标签。

Result: 在MMLU、TriviaQA和GSM8K基准测试上，CMP和CME均优于模型内不确定性基线。在MMLU上，CMP达到0.75的平均AUROC，而模型内熵基线仅为0.59。

Conclusion: 跨模型分歧是一种实用、无需训练的无标签正确性估计方法，可直接应用于部署监控、模型路由、选择性预测、数据过滤和生产语言模型系统的可扩展监督。

Abstract: Detecting when a language model is wrong without ground truth labels is a fundamental challenge for safe deployment. Existing approaches rely on a model's own uncertainty -- such as token entropy or confidence scores -- but these signals fail critically on the most dangerous failure mode: confident errors, where a model is wrong but certain. In this work we introduce cross-model disagreement as a correctness indicator -- a simple, training-free signal that can be dropped into existing production systems, routing pipelines, and deployment monitoring infrastructure without modification. Given a model's generated answer, cross-model disagreement computes how surprised or uncertain a second verifier model is when reading that answer via a single forward pass. No generation from the verifying model is required, and no correctness labels are needed. We instantiate this principle as Cross-Model Perplexity (CMP), which measures the verifying model's surprise at the generating model's answer tokens, and Cross-Model Entropy (CME), which measures the verifying model's uncertainty at those positions. Both CMP and CME outperform within-model uncertainty baselines across benchmarks spanning reasoning, retrieval, and mathematical problem solving (MMLU, TriviaQA, and GSM8K). On MMLU, CMP achieves a mean AUROC of 0.75 against a within-model entropy baseline of 0.59. These results establish cross-model disagreement as a practical, training-free approach to label-free correctness estimation, with direct applications in deployment monitoring, model routing, selective prediction, data filtering, and scalable oversight of production language model systems.

</details>


### [109] [Retraining as Approximate Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25480)
*Harrison Katz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于决策理论的模型重训练框架，将重训练决策转化为成本最小化问题，用证据驱动的触发器替代传统的日历调度，实现了可审计的治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，模型重训练被视为持续的维护任务，缺乏理论指导。作者认为重训练可以更好地被理解为计算约束下的近似贝叶斯推断，填补了持续更新的信念状态与冻结部署模型之间的差距（即“学习债务”），从而为重训练决策提供理论基础。

Method: 构建了一个决策理论框架，将重训练决策建模为成本最小化问题，其阈值由损失函数自然导出。定义了“学习债务”概念来量化模型状态与理想信念状态的差距，并设计了基于证据的触发机制来决定何时进行重训练。

Result: 开发了基于证据的重训练触发器，用以替代传统的日历调度方法。这种触发机制使重训练决策具有可审计性，为模型治理提供了透明、可追溯的框架。

Conclusion: 重训练决策应当从经验性的维护任务转变为基于决策理论的系统化过程。通过将重训练理解为近似贝叶斯推断，可以实现更科学、可审计的重训练策略，提升机器学习系统的治理水平。

Abstract: Model retraining is usually treated as an ongoing maintenance task. But as Harrison Katz now argues, retraining can be better understood as approximate Bayesian inference under computational constraints. The gap between a continuously updated belief state and your frozen deployed model is "learning debt," and the retraining decision is a cost minimization problem with a threshold that falls out of your loss function. In this article Katz provides a decision-theoretic framework for retraining policies. The result is evidence-based triggers that replace calendar schedules and make governance auditable. For readers less familiar with the Bayesian and decision-theoretic language, key terms are defined in a glossary at the end of the article.

</details>


### [110] [EcoThink: A Green Adaptive Inference Framework for Sustainable and Accessible Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25498)
*Linxiao Li,Zhixiang Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了EcoThink框架，通过动态评估查询复杂度来减少LLM不必要的推理，平均降低40.4%的推理能耗，且不显著影响性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM对数十亿次日常查询不加区分地应用链式思维（CoT）等计算密集型策略，导致模型“过度思考”、资源浪费和碳排放增加，这违背了联合国可持续发展目标（气候行动与减少不平等），阻碍了资源受限地区公平获取AI。

Method: 提出EcoThink，一种能源感知的自适应推理框架。该方法使用轻量级的、基于蒸馏的路由器动态评估查询复杂度，对事实性检索类查询跳过不必要的推理步骤，仅对复杂逻辑任务保留深度计算。

Result: 在9个多样化基准测试上的评估表明，EcoThink平均减少40.4%的推理能耗（在Web知识检索任务中最高可降81.9%），且未导致统计上显著的性能损失。

Conclusion: EcoThink通过减少算法层面的计算浪费，为构建可持续、包容且能效高的生成式AI智能体提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: As the Web transitions from static retrieval to generative interaction, the escalating environmental footprint of Large Language Models (LLMs) presents a critical sustainability challenge. Current paradigms indiscriminately apply computation-intensive strategies like Chain-of-Thought (CoT) to billions of daily queries, causing LLM overthinking, a redundancy that amplifies carbon emissions and operational barriers. This inefficiency directly undermines UN Sustainable Development Goals 13 (Climate Action) and 10 (Reduced Inequalities) by hindering equitable AI access in resource-constrained regions. To address this, we introduce EcoThink, an energy-aware adaptive inference framework designed to reconcile high-performance AI intelligence with environmental responsibility. EcoThink employs a lightweight, distillation-based router to dynamically assess query complexity, skipping unnecessary reasoning for factoid retrieval while reserving deep computation for complex logic. Extensive evaluations across 9 diverse benchmarks demonstrate that EcoThink reduces inference energy by 40.4% on average (up to 81.9% for web knowledge retrieval) without statistically significant performance loss. By mitigating algorithmic waste, EcoThink offers a scalable path toward a sustainable, inclusive, and energy-efficient generative AI Agent.

</details>


### [111] [Voxtral TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25551)
*Alexander H. Liu,Alexis Tacnet,Andy Ehrenberg,Andy Lo,Chen-Yo Sun,Guillaume Lample,Henry Lagarde,Jean-Malo Delignon,Jaeyoung Kim,John Harvill,Khyathi Raghavi Chandu,Lorenzo Signoretti,Margaret Jennings,Patrick von Platen,Pavankumar Reddy Muddireddy,Rohin Arora,Sanchit Gandhi,Samuel Humeau,Soham Ghosh,Srijan Mishra,Van Phung,Abdelaziz Bounhar,Abhinav Rastogi,Adrien Sadé,Alan Jeffares,Albert Jiang,Alexandre Cahill,Alexandre Gavaudan,Alexandre Sablayrolles,Amélie Héliou,Amos You,Andrew Bai,Andrew Zhao,Angele Lenglemetz,Anmol Agarwal,Anton Eliseev,Antonia Calvi,Arjun Majumdar,Arthur Fournier,Artjom Joosen,Avi Sooriyarachchi,Aysenur Karaduman Utkur,Baptiste Bout,Baptiste Rozière,Baudouin De Monicault,Benjamin Tibi,Bowen Yang,Charlotte Cronjäger,Clémence Lanfranchi,Connor Chen,Corentin Barreau,Corentin Sautier,Cyprien Courtot,Darius Dabert,Diego de las Casas,Elizaveta Demyanenko,Elliot Chane-Sane,Emmanuel Gottlob,Enguerrand Paquin,Etienne Goffinet,Fabien Niel,Faruk Ahmed,Federico Baldassarre,Gabrielle Berrada,Gaëtan Ecrepont,Gauthier Guinet,Genevieve Hayes,Georgii Novikov,Giada Pistilli,Guillaume Kunsch,Guillaume Martin,Guillaume Raille,Gunjan Dhanuka,Gunshi Gupta,Han Zhou,Harshil Shah,Hope McGovern,Hugo Thimonier,Indraneel Mukherjee,Irene Zhang,Jacques Sun,Jan Ludziejewski,Jason Rute,Jérémie Dentan,Joachim Studnia,Jonas Amar,Joséphine Delas,Josselin Somerville Roberts,Julien Tauran,Karmesh Yadav,Kartik Khandelwal,Kilian Tep,Kush Jain,Laurence Aitchison,Laurent Fainsin,Léonard Blier,Lingxiao Zhao,Louis Martin,Lucile Saulnier,Luyu Gao,Maarten Buyl,Manan Sharma,Marie Pellat,Mark Prins,Martin Alexandre,Mathieu Poirée,Mathieu Schmitt,Mathilde Guillaumin,Matthieu Dinot,Matthieu Futeral,Maxime Darrin,Maximilian Augustin,Mert Unsal,Mia Chiquier,Mikhail Biriuchinskii,Minh-Quang Pham,Mircea Lica,Morgane Rivière,Nathan Grinsztajn,Neha Gupta,Olivier Bousquet,Olivier Duchenne,Patricia Wang,Paul Jacob,Paul Wambergue,Paula Kurylowicz,Philippe Pinel,Philomène Chagniot,Pierre Stock,Piotr Miłoś,Prateek Gupta,Pravesh Agrawal,Quentin Torroba,Ram Ramrakhya,Randall Isenhour,Rishi Shah,Romain Sauvestre,Roman Soletskyi,Rosalie Millner,Rupert Menneer,Sagar Vaze,Samuel Barry,Samuel Belkadi,Sandeep Subramanian,Sean Cha,Shashwat Verma,Siddhant Waghjale,Siddharth Gandhi,Simon Lepage,Sumukh Aithal,Szymon Antoniak,Tarun Kumar Vangani,Teven Le Scao,Théo Cachet,Theo Simon Sorg,Thibaut Lavril,Thomas Chabal,Thomas Foubert,Thomas Robert,Thomas Wang,Tim Lawson,Tom Bewley,Tom Edwards,Tyler Wang,Umar Jamil,Umberto Tomasini,Valeriia Nemychnikova,Vedant Nanda,Victor Jouault,Vincent Maladière,Vincent Pfister,Virgile Richard,Vladislav Bataev,Wassim Bouaziz,Wen-Ding Li,William Havard,William Marshall,Xinghui Li,Xingran Guo,Xinyu Yang,Yannic Neuhaus,Yassine El Ouahidi,Yassir Bendou,Yihan Wang,Yimu Pan,Zaccharie Ramzi,Zhenlin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Voxtral TTS，一种仅需3秒参考音频即可生成自然语音的表达性多语言文本转语音模型，在人工评测中以68.4%的胜率超越ElevenLabs Flash v2.5，并已开源模型权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个能够高效进行多语言语音克隆的表达性文本转语音模型，解决现有方法在自然度、表现力以及对参考音频数据需求方面的不足。

Method: 采用混合架构，首先利用自回归模型生成语义语音标记，随后使用流匹配（flow-matching）方法生成声学标记。模型配合Voxtral Codec语音分词器使用，该分词器采用混合VQ-FSQ量化方案从头训练。

Result: 在由母语者进行的人工评测中，Voxtral TTS在多语言语音克隆的自然度与表现力方面表现优异，相比ElevenLabs Flash v2.5获得了68.4%的胜率。模型仅需3秒参考音频即可工作。

Conclusion: Voxtral TTS成功实现了高效、表达性强的多语言语音克隆，验证了其混合架构的有效性，并以CC BY-NC许可公开发布了模型权重。

Abstract: We introduce Voxtral TTS, an expressive multilingual text-to-speech model that generates natural speech from as little as 3 seconds of reference audio. Voxtral TTS adopts a hybrid architecture that combines auto-regressive generation of semantic speech tokens with flow-matching for acoustic tokens. These tokens are encoded and decoded with Voxtral Codec, a speech tokenizer trained from scratch with a hybrid VQ-FSQ quantization scheme. In human evaluations conducted by native speakers, Voxtral TTS is preferred for multilingual voice cloning due to its naturalness and expressivity, achieving a 68.4\% win rate over ElevenLabs Flash v2.5. We release the model weights under a CC BY-NC license.

</details>


### [112] [Is Mathematical Problem-Solving Expertise in Large Language Models Associated with Assessment Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25633)
*Liang Zhang,Yu Fu,Xinyi Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究发现大语言模型的数学解题能力与其步骤级评估表现正相关：模型对自己正确解答的问题进行错误诊断的准确率显著更高。然而，评估任务仍比直接解题更难，表明可靠的步骤级诊断需要额外的能力（如步骤追踪和错误定位）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学教育中不仅被用作问题解决者，还被用作学习者推理的评估者。然而，目前尚不清楚更强的数学问题解决能力是否与更强的步骤级评估表现相关联，这一关系对AI辅导系统的设计至关重要。

Method: 使用PROCESSBENCH数据集的GSM8K和MATH子集（人工标注的数学推理错误步骤识别基准），评估基于GPT-4和GPT-5的两种LLM数学辅导智能体设置。在相同的数学问题上执行两个独立任务：解决原始问题；评估基准提供的解答并预测最早的错误步骤。

Result: 发现一致的模型内模式：模型在正确解答的问题上的评估准确率显著高于其错误解答的问题，两个模型和数据集上的关联均具有统计显著性。同时，评估任务仍比直接解题更困难，特别是在存在错误的解答上。

Conclusion: 数学问题解决专长有助于更强的评估表现，但可靠的步骤级诊断还需要额外能力，如步骤追踪、监控和精确的错误定位。研究结果对设计和评估用于数学教育形成性评估的AI支持自适应教学系统具有重要启示。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in math education not only as problem solvers but also as assessors of learners' reasoning. However, it remains unclear whether stronger math problem-solving ability is associated with stronger step-level assessment performance. This study examines that relationship using the GSM8K and MATH subsets of PROCESSBENCH, a human-annotated benchmark for identifying the earliest erroneous step in mathematical reasoning. We evaluate two LLM-based math tutor agent settings, instantiated with GPT-4 and GPT-5, in two independent tasks on the same math problems: solving the original problem and assessing a benchmark-provided solution by predicting the earliest erroneous step. Results show a consistent within-model pattern: assessment accuracy is substantially higher on math problem items the same model solved correctly than on items it solved incorrectly, with statistically significant associations across both models and datasets. At the same time, assessment remains more difficult than direct problem solving, especially on error-present solutions. These findings suggest that math problem-solving expertise supports stronger assessment performance, but reliable step-level diagnosis also requires additional capabilities such as step tracking, monitoring, and precise error localization. The results have implications for the design and evaluation of AI-supported Adaptive Instructional Systems (AISs) for formative assessment in math education.

</details>


### [113] [R-C2: Cycle-Consistent Reinforcement Learning Improves Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25720)
*Zirui Zhang,Haoyu Dong,Kexin Pei,Chengzhi Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RC2强化学习框架，利用跨模态循环一致性作为无标签奖励信号，解决多模态模型内部的预测矛盾问题，显著提升推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态模型常对同一概念的视觉和文本表示产生矛盾的预测，而现有的投票机制掩盖了这些失败甚至放大了系统偏差。作者认为跨模态不一致性本身就是一种丰富且自然的学习信号，不应被掩盖，而应被利用来解决内部冲突。

Method: 引入RC2（Reinforced Cross-modal Cycle Consistency）强化学习框架。该方法强制模型执行反向推理、切换模态，并通过前向推理可靠地重建答案，以此构建跨模态循环一致性约束。这种循环约束提供了一个稠密且无需标签的奖励信号，促使模型自主对齐其内部表示。

Result: 优化该结构有效缓解了模态特定的错误，推理准确性最高提升了7.6个百分点。结果表明高级推理能力不仅源于数据规模的扩大，还源于对世界结构一致性的强制执行。

Conclusion: 高级推理能力的涌现不仅依赖于数据规模的扩展，更依赖于强制模型建立对世界结构一致的理解；通过利用而非掩盖跨模态不一致性，可以有效提升模型的鲁棒性与推理表现。

Abstract: Robust perception and reasoning require consistency across sensory modalities. Yet current multimodal models often violate this principle, yielding contradictory predictions for visual and textual representations of the same concept. Rather than masking these failures with standard voting mechanisms, which can amplify systematic biases, we show that cross-modal inconsistency provides a rich and natural signal for learning. We introduce RC2, a reinforcement learning framework that resolves internal conflicts by enforcing cross-modal cycle consistency. By requiring a model to perform backward inference, switch modalities, and reliably reconstruct the answer through forward inference, we obtain a dense, label-free reward. This cyclic constraint encourages the model to align its internal representations autonomously. Optimizing for this structure mitigates modality-specific errors and improves reasoning accuracy by up to 7.6 points. Our results suggest that advanced reasoning emerges not only from scaling data, but also from enforcing a structurally consistent understanding of the world.

</details>


### [114] [Back to Basics: Revisiting ASR in the Age of Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25727)
*Geeyang Tay,Wentao Ma,Jaewon Lee,Yuzhi Tang,Daniel Lee,Weisu Yin,Dongming Shen,Silin Meng,Yi Zhu,Mu Li,Alex Smola*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了WildASR多语言诊断基准，发现现有ASR系统在真实场景下性能严重下降且不均匀，模型常产生幻觉内容，存在安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR系统虽然在基准测试中接近人类水平，但在真实语音代理应用中仍频繁失败，且缺乏能够隔离特定失败因素的诊断工具，导致从业者无法预测系统在不同语言和条件下的性能退化程度。

Method: 构建了WildASR多语言（四种语言）诊断基准，数据完全来源于真实人类语音，将ASR鲁棒性沿三个维度进行分解：环境退化、人口统计偏移和语言多样性。对七个广泛使用的ASR系统进行评估，并开发了三个分析工具辅助部署决策。

Result: 发现ASR系统存在严重且不均匀的性能退化，模型鲁棒性无法跨语言或跨条件迁移。关键发现是模型在部分或退化输入下常产生幻觉，生成合理但未被说出的内容，这对下游代理行为构成具体的安全风险。

Conclusion: 针对性的、因素隔离的评估对于理解和改进生产系统中ASR的可靠性至关重要。该研究不仅提供了基准测试，还为从业者提供了分析工具以指导部署决策。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) systems have achieved near-human accuracy on curated benchmarks, yet still fail in real-world voice agents under conditions that current evaluations do not systematically cover. Without diagnostic tools that isolate specific failure factors, practitioners cannot anticipate which conditions, in which languages, will cause what degree of degradation. We introduce WildASR, a multilingual (four-language) diagnostic benchmark sourced entirely from real human speech that factorizes ASR robustness along three axes: environmental degradation, demographic shift, and linguistic diversity. Evaluating seven widely used ASR systems, we find severe and uneven performance degradation, and model robustness does not transfer across languages or conditions. Critically, models often hallucinate plausible but unspoken content under partial or degraded inputs, creating concrete safety risks for downstream agent behavior. Our results demonstrate that targeted, factor-isolated evaluation is essential for understanding and improving ASR reliability in production systems. Besides the benchmark itself, we also present three analytical tools that practitioners can use to guide deployment decisions.

</details>


### [115] [Training the Knowledge Base through Evidence Distillation and Write-Back Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25737)
*Yuxing Lu,Xukai Zhao,Wei Wu,Jinzhuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出WriteBack-RAG框架，将RAG系统中的知识库视为可训练组件，通过离线提炼相关文档为紧凑知识单元来优化语料库，在所有评估设置中平均提升2.14%，且可跨方法迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统的知识库通常一次性构建后不再修改，但查询所需的事实往往分散在文档中并被无关内容掩埋，导致检索效果受限。

Method: 提出WriteBack-RAG框架，利用标注样本识别检索成功位置、隔离相关文档，将其提炼为紧凑知识单元并与原始语料库一起索引。该方法仅修改语料库，可作为离线预处理步骤应用于任何RAG流程。

Result: 在4种RAG方法、6个基准测试和2个LLM骨干网络上，WriteBack-RAG在所有评估设置中均取得提升，平均增益+2.14%。跨方法迁移实验表明，提炼的知识可惠及生成时使用的RAG流程之外的其他流程，证明改进源于语料库本身。

Conclusion: 将知识库视为可训练组件是有效的，通过语料库优化可实现与RAG方法无关的性能提升，验证了改进的可迁移性和通用性。

Abstract: The knowledge base in a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system is typically assembled once and never revised, even though the facts a query requires are often fragmented across documents and buried in irrelevant content. We argue that the knowledge base should be treated as a trainable component and propose WriteBack-RAG, a framework that uses labeled examples to identify where retrieval succeeds, isolate the relevant documents, and distill them into compact knowledge units that are indexed alongside the original corpus. Because the method modifies only the corpus, it can be applied once as an offline preprocessing step and combined with any RAG pipeline. Across four RAG methods, six benchmarks, and two LLM backbones, WriteBack-RAG improves every evaluated setting, with gains averaging +2.14%. Cross-method transfer experiments further show that the distilled knowledge benefits RAG pipelines other than the one used to produce it, confirming that the improvement resides in the corpus itself.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Decentralized Task Scheduling in Distributed Systems: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24738)
*Daniel Benniah John*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于NumPy轻量级实现的去中心化多智能体深度强化学习（DRL-MADRL）框架，用于解决异构分布式系统中的任务调度问题，在平均任务完成时间、能效和SLA满意度上分别取得了15.6%、15.2%和显著优于基线的成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模分布式系统中，由于动态工作负载、异构资源和竞争性服务质量要求，任务调度面临巨大挑战。传统的中心化方法存在可扩展性限制和单点故障问题，而经典启发式算法缺乏对变化环境的适应能力。

Method: 将任务调度问题建模为去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDP），开发了仅需NumPy、Matplotlib和SciPy实现的轻量级Actor-Critic架构。该框架可部署在资源受限的边缘设备上，无需依赖重量级机器学习框架，实现了去中心化的多智能体深度强化学习。

Result: 在100节点异构系统上进行的30次实验（每轮处理1000个任务）表明：平均任务完成时间相比随机基线改善15.6%（30.8秒 vs 36.5秒）；能效提升15.2%（745.2 kWh vs 878.3 kWh）；SLA满意度达到82.3%，显著优于基线的75.5%。所有改进均具有统计显著性（p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: 该研究成功证明了去中心化多智能体深度强化学习框架在异构分布式任务调度中的有效性，实现了性能、能效和服务质量的多重优化。轻量级实现方案使其适合在资源受限的边缘设备上部署，为分布式系统调度提供了可扩展、高适应性的解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient task scheduling in large-scale distributed systems presents significant challenges due to dynamic workloads, heterogeneous resources, and competing quality-of-service requirements. Traditional centralized approaches face scalability limitations and single points of failure, while classical heuristics lack adaptability to changing conditions. This paper proposes a decentralized multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (DRL-MADRL) framework for task scheduling in heterogeneous distributed systems. We formulate the problem as a Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (Dec-POMDP) and develop a lightweight actor-critic architecture implemented using only NumPy, enabling deployment on resource-constrained edge devices without heavyweight machine learning frameworks. Using workload characteristics derived from the publicly available Google Cluster Trace dataset, we evaluate our approach on a 100-node heterogeneous system processing 1,000 tasks per episode over 30 experimental runs. Experimental results demonstrate 15.6% improvement in average task completion time (30.8s vs 36.5s for random baseline), 15.2% energy efficiency gain (745.2 kWh vs 878.3 kWh), and 82.3% SLA satisfaction compared to 75.5% for baselines, with all improvements statistically significant (p < 0.001). The lightweight implementation requires only NumPy, Matplotlib, and SciPy. Complete source code and experimental data are provided for full reproducibility at https://github.com/danielbenniah/marl-distributed-scheduling.

</details>


### [117] [Rafture: Erasure-coded Raft with Post-Dissemination Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24761)
*Rithwik Kerur,Divyakant Agrawal,Michael K. Reiter,Dahlia Malkhi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Rafture，一种新型信息 dispersal 算法及其在共识协议中的集成。它是首个引入传播后剪枝机制的解决方案，允许系统在传播完成后自适应调整存储成本，同时简化了恢复过程并显著改善了动态网络环境下的长期存储消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要关注传播时的效率，但导致恢复过程复杂（需要因共识值而异的元数据）、依赖中心化领导者进行存储决策，且无法在传播后调整存储成本。在实际部署中，网络延迟不可预测，特别是当信息 dispersal 集成到延迟敏感的共识协议时，需要一种既能高效传播又能灵活调整存储的解决方案。

Method: Rafture 采用简单的固定阈值纠删码，沿第二维度变化不同的分片分配。这种方法确保始终可以通过F+1个分片使用相同的插值方法进行重建，无需额外元数据。关键创新是引入传播后剪枝机制，允许节点根据本地观察的信息自主调整存储策略，消除了全局协调的需求。

Result: 在高度动态的网络环境评估中，Rafture 显著改善了可变网络条件下的长期存储消耗，同时简化了恢复过程。该算法能够在传播完成后自适应调整存储成本，避免了复杂恢复过程对额外元数据的依赖。

Conclusion: Rafture 成功克服了现有信息 dispersal 解决方案的关键限制，通过传播后剪枝机制实现了存储成本的自适应调整，同时保持了简单的恢复过程。这为分布式系统中纠删码数据的高效传播和存储提供了一个实用的解决方案，特别是在网络条件不可预测的实际部署环境中。

Abstract: Spreading and storing erasure-coded data in distributed systems effectively is challenging in real settings. Practical deployments must contend with unpredictable network latencies, particularly when information dispersal is integrated into consensus protocols, a prominent and latency-sensitive use case. Existing approaches address this challenge through timeout-based dissemination and adaptive communication or storage decisions driven by acknowledgments during dissemination. However, these designs focus almost exclusively on dissemination-time efficiency, complicate recovery with reconstruction procedures that require metadata that can differ per consensus value, and rely on a centralized leader to make storage decisions for all nodes.
  This paper introduces \textbf{Rafture}, a novel information dispersal algorithm, and its integration in a consensus protocol, that overcomes these limitations. Rafture is the first information dispersal solution to incorporate post-dissemination pruning, allowing systems to adapt storage costs after dissemination completes. It employs a simple, fixed-threshold erasure code while varying distinct fragment assignment along a second dimension. This ensures that reconstruction is always possible from $F+1$ fragments using the same interpolation method and no additional metadata. Rafture further enables nodes to adapt autonomously based on locally observed information, eliminating the need for global coordination.
  We evaluate Rafture in highly dynamic network settings and show that it simplifies recovery while significantly improving long-term storage consumption under variable network conditions.

</details>


### [118] [eBeeMetrics: An eBPF-based Library Framework for Feedback-free Observability of QoS Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25067)
*Muntaka Ibnath,Mohammadreza Rezvani,Daniel Wong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出eBeeMetrics，一个基于eBPF的框架，通过观测系统调用等内核事件，实现对应用级QoS指标（如尾延迟、吞吐量）的准确推断，从而简化系统管理运行时与QoS反馈机制的集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多系统管理运行时（如资源管理和功耗管理技术）依赖QoS指标（尾延迟、吞吐量）作为反馈，但这些指标通常无法通过硬件性能计数器观测，也无法在操作系统内核中直接观测。这导致在应用程序插桩以及将QoS性能指标反馈与多种管理运行时集成时引入了复杂性和开销。

Method: 引入eBeeMetrics，一个基于eBPF的库框架，仅利用eBPF可观测的事件（如系统调用）来准确推导应用级指标。该框架可作为即插即用的替代方案，将系统管理运行时与QoS指标反馈报告解耦，或补充现有QoS指标以更好地识别服务端动态。

Result: eBeeMetrics在多种延迟敏感的工作负载下，与真实测量的吞吐量和延迟指标实现了强相关性，证明了其准确性和有效性。工具已开源。

Conclusion: eBeeMetrics成功弥合了内核级可观测事件与应用级QoS指标之间的差距，为系统管理运行时提供了一种低开销、易集成的QoS指标观测方案。

Abstract: Many system management runtimes (SMRs), such as resource management and power management techniques, rely on quality-of-service (QoS) metrics, such as tail latency or throughput, as feedback. These QoS metrics are generally neither observable with hardware performance counters nor directly observable within the OS kernel. This introduces complexity and overhead in instrumenting the application and integrating QoS performance metric feedback with many management runtimes. To bridge this gap, we introduced eBeeMetrics, an eBPF-based library framework to accurately observe application-level metrics derived from only eBPF-observable events, such as system calls. eBeeMetrics can be used as a drop-in replacement to decouple system management runtimes from QoS metric feedback reporting, or can supplement existing QoS metrics to better identify server-side dynamics. eBeeMetrics achieves a strong correlation with real-world measured throughput and latency metrics across various latency-sensitive workloads. The eBeeMetrics tool is open-source; the source code is available at: https://github.com/Ibnathism/eBeeMetrics.

</details>


### [119] [DFLOP: A Data-driven Framework for Multimodal LLM Training Pipeline Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25120)
*Hyeonjun An,Sihyun Kim,Chaerim Lim,Hyunjoon Kim,Rathijit Sen,Sangmin Jung,Hyeonsoo Lee,Dongwook Kim,Takki Yu,Jinkyu Jeong,Youngsok Kim,Kwanghyun Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DFLOP，一个数据驱动的多模态大语言模型训练流水线优化框架，通过感知输入数据特征来解决现有分布式训练中的计算偏差问题，实现了最高3.6倍的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态大语言模型分布式训练框架存在“数据盲区”问题：它们在并行化计算时未考虑输入数据特征的变化。由于异构的多模态输入会产生不同的处理成本，这种数据无感知性会导致严重的计算偏差、GPU资源利用率不均、同步延迟累积以及整体训练效率下降。

Method: 提出DFLOP框架，该框架持续分析运行时行为以捕获由数据引起的计算差异，并采用预测性调度来平衡各阶段和微批次间的工作负载。通过将数据特征与执行规划相结合来优化训练流程。

Result: 在大规模多模态基准测试上的广泛实验表明，DFLOP相比最先进的分布式训练框架实现了最高3.6倍的训练加速，显著提升了GPU利用率和吞吐量。

Conclusion: DFLOP成功解决了多模态大语言模型训练中的数据无感知问题，通过数据驱动的流水线优化方法，有效平衡了工作负载并提升了训练效率，证明了将数据特征纳入执行规划的重要性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable advances by integrating text, image, and audio understanding within a unified architecture. However, existing distributed training frameworks remain fundamentally data-blind: they parallelize computation without accounting for variations in input data characteristics. This data unawareness leads to severe computation skew across stages and microbatches, where heterogeneous multimodal inputs incur different processing costs. Consequently, GPU resources are unevenly utilized, synchronization delays accumulate, and overall training efficiency degrades. To address this limitation, we present DFLOP, a data-driven framework for multimodal LLM training pipeline optimization. DFLOP continuously profiles runtime behavior to capture data-induced computation variance and employs predictive scheduling to balance workloads across stages and microbatches. By coupling data characteristics with execution planning, DFLOP substantially improves GPU utilization and throughput. Extensive experiments on large-scale multimodal benchmarks show that DFLOP achieves up to 3.6x faster training compared to state-of-the-art distributed training frameworks.

</details>


### [120] [Revealing the influence of participant failures on model quality in cross-silo Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25289)
*Fabian Stricker,David Bermbach,Christian Zirpins*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了联邦学习（FL）中因故障导致的参与者缺失对模型性能的影响，发现数据偏斜是关键因素，会导致模型评估过度乐观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在生产环境中需要具备可靠性，但作为一个分布式系统，它易受崩溃故障、网络分区等问题影响导致参与者缺失。目前学术界尚未系统研究此类故障对联邦学习结果的具体影响，本文旨在填补这一研究空白。

Method: 通过在图像、表格和时间序列数据上进行大量实验，系统地分析了参与者缺失对模型性能的影响。研究考虑了多种影响因素，包括数据偏斜、不同的可用性模式以及模型架构，并考察了特定场景（如全局模型对缺失参与者的效用）。

Result: 实验详细揭示了各影响因素的作用。关键发现表明，数据偏斜对结果有强烈影响，往往导致模型评估过度乐观，在某些情况下甚至会改变其他影响因素的效果。

Conclusion: 参与者缺失对联邦学习模型性能有显著影响，其中数据偏斜是核心影响因素。研究结果强调了在设计和部署联邦学习系统时，必须充分考虑故障场景和参与者缺失问题，以确保学习的有效性、稳定性和可复现性。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) is a paradigm for training machine learning (ML) models in collaborative settings while preserving participants' privacy by keeping raw data local. A key requirement for the use of FL in production is reliability, as insufficient reliability can compromise the validity, stability, and reproducibility of learning outcomes. FL inherently operates as a distributed system and is therefore susceptible to crash failures, network partitioning, and other fault scenarios. Despite this, the impact of such failures on FL outcomes has not yet been studied systematically.
  In this paper, we address this gap by investigating the impact of missing participants in FL. To this end, we conduct extensive experiments on image, tabular, and time-series data and analyze how the absence of participants affects model performance, taking into account influencing factors such as data skewness, different availability patterns, and model architectures. Furthermore, we examine scenario-specific aspects, including the utility of the global model for missing participants. Our experiments provide detailed insights into the effects of various influencing factors. In particular, we show that data skewness has a strong impact, often leading to overly optimistic model evaluations and, in some cases, even altering the effects of other influencing factors.

</details>


### [121] [The Complexity of Distributed Minimum Weight Cycle Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25368)
*Yi-Jun Chang,Yanyu Chen,Dipan Dey,Yonggang Jiang,Gopinath Mishra,Hung Thuan Nguyen,Mingyang Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在CONGEST分布式计算模型下研究了最小权重环问题，提出了近似比与轮复杂度之间的平滑权衡机制，并在特定条件下证明了紧匹配的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究最小权重环（MWC）问题在分布式CONGEST模型中的计算复杂度，此前的工作由Manoharan和Ramachandran（PODC 2024）提出了(2+ε)-近似算法，但近似比与轮复杂度之间的权衡关系尚不明确，需要更精细的理论界限。

Method: 针对无向加权图设计了随机化算法，通过对任意实数k≥1实现(k+1)-近似，推导出包含节点数n和直径D的轮复杂度公式；同时在Erdős girth猜想假设下，针对任意整数k≥1证明了(k+1-ε)-近似算法的轮数下界。

Result: 上界：算法达到(k+1)-近似，轮复杂度为Õ(n^(k+1)/(2k+1) + n^(1/k) + D·n^(1/2(2k+1)) + D^(2/5)n^(2/5+1/2(2k+1)))，当k≥2且D=Õ(n^(1/4))时简化为Õ(n^(k+1)/(2k+1))。下界：在Erdős girth猜想下，(k+1-ε)-近似需要Ω̃(n^(k+1)/(2k+1))轮，适用于有向无权图和无向加权图，即使直径很小（D=Θ(log n)）。

Conclusion: 当k≥2且直径较小（D=Õ(n^(1/4))）时，上下界在多项式对数因子内紧匹配，得出了最小权重环问题分布式复杂度的近似紧确界，显著改进了现有技术水平。

Abstract: We investigate the \emph{minimum weight cycle (MWC)} problem in the $\mathsf{CONGEST}$ model of distributed computing.
  For undirected weighted graphs, we design a randomized algorithm that achieves a $(k+1)$-approximation, for any \emph{real} number $k \ge 1$. The round complexity of algorithm is \[ \tilde{O}\!\Big( n^{\frac{k+1}{2k+1}} + n^{\frac{1}{k}} + D\, n^{\frac{1}{2(2k+1)}} + D^{\frac{2}{5}} n^{\frac{2}{5}+\frac{1}{2(2k+1)}} \Big). \] where $n$ denotes the number of nodes and $D$ is the unweighted diameter of the graph. This result yields a smooth trade-off between approximation ratio and round complexity. In particular, when $k \geq 2$ and $D = \tilde{O}(n^{1/4})$, the bound simplifies to \[ \tilde{O}\!\left(
  n^{\frac{k+1}{2k+1}} \right) \]
  On the lower bound side, assuming the Erdős girth conjecture, we prove that for every \emph{integer} $k \ge 1$, any randomized $(k+1-ε)$-approximation algorithm for MWC requires \[ \tildeΩ\!\left(
  n^{\frac{k+1}{2k+1}} \right) \] rounds. This lower bound holds for both directed unweighted and undirected weighted graphs, and applies even to graphs with small diameter $D = Θ(\log n)$.
  Taken together, our upper and lower bounds \emph{match up to polylogarithmic factors} for graphs of sufficiently small diameter $D = \tilde{O}(n^{1/4})$ (when $k \geq 2$), yielding a nearly tight bound on the distributed complexity of the problem.
  Our results improve upon the previous state of the art: Manoharan and Ramachandran (PODC~2024) demonstrated a $(2+ε)$-approximation algorithm for undirected weighted graphs with round complexity $\tilde{O}(n^{2/3}+D)$, and proved that for any arbitrarily large number $α$, any $α$-approximation algorithm for directed unweighted or undirected weighted graphs requires $Ω(\sqrt{n}/\log n)$ rounds.

</details>


### [122] [PRISM: Dynamic Primitive-Based Forecasting for Large-Scale GPU Cluster Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25378)
*Xin Wu,Fei Teng,Xingwang Li,Bin Zheng,Qiang Duan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了PRISM框架，一种基于原语的组合式预测方法，用于解决GPU工作负载预测问题，在处理现代工作负载的高波动性和多周期性方面达到了最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU工作负载的准确预测对AI基础设施的调度、资源分配和功耗管理至关重要。然而，现代工作负载具有高波动性、多周期性和异构性等特点，使得传统预测器难以有效应对，亟需一种能够处理这些复杂特性的新方法。

Method: 提出PRISM（Primitive-based compositional forecasting framework），采用双表示方法：结合字典驱动的时间分解与自适应频谱细化。该方法能够从多样化的GPU作业中提取稳定且可解释的工作负载特征签名。

Result: 在大规模生产追踪数据上进行评估，PRISM取得了最先进的结果。该方法显著降低了突发阶段的预测误差，为GPU驱动的AI平台动态资源管理提供了坚实基础。

Conclusion: PRISM为GPU工作负载预测提供了一种鲁棒的、架构感知的基础方法，能够有效应对现代工作负载的复杂特性，对AI基础设施的动态资源管理具有重要价值。

Abstract: Accurately forecasting GPU workloads is essential for AI infrastructure, enabling efficient scheduling, resource allocation, and power management. Modern workloads are highly volatile, multiple periodicity, and heterogeneous, making them challenging for traditional predictors. We propose PRISM, a primitive-based compositional forecasting framework combining dictionary-driven temporal decomposition with adaptive spectral refinement. This dual representation extracts stable, interpretable workload signatures across diverse GPU jobs. Evaluated on large-scale production traces, PRISM achieves state-of-the-art results. It significantly reduces burst-phase errors, providing a robust, architecture-aware foundation for dynamic resource management in GPU-powered AI platforms.

</details>


### [123] [SHADOW: Seamless Handoff And Zero-Downtime Orchestrated Workload Migration for Stateful Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25484)
*Hai Dinh-Tuan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SHADOW框架，通过ShadowPod策略和身份交换机制，实现了Kubernetes中有状态微服务的零停机迁移，将迁移时间缩短77%并消除服务中断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes中的有状态微服务迁移面临严峻挑战：容器重启会导致内存状态丢失，而StatefulSet的身份约束禁止相同序号的Pod同时运行，迫使采用串行的停止-重建循环，导致中位数38.5秒的服务停机时间。现有方案无法在保证状态一致性的同时实现零停机迁移。

Method: 提出SHADOW框架，实现基于消息的有状态微服务迁移(MS2M)方法作为Kubernetes Operator。核心技术包括：(1) ShadowPod策略：在目标节点从CRIU检查点镜像创建影子Pod，同时源Pod继续服务，允许消息回放期间并发运行；(2) 针对StatefulSet的身份交换过程：使用ExchangeFence机制对影子Pod重新检查点，创建StatefulSet拥有的替代Pod，排空两个消息队列以确保切换期间零消息丢失。

Result: 在裸机Kubernetes集群上进行280次迁移实验，覆盖4种配置和7种消息速率(10-120msg/s)。结果表明：与StatefulSet工作负载的串行基线相比，ShadowPod策略将恢复阶段缩短92%，完全消除服务停机，总迁移时间减少77%，且所有280次迁移均实现零消息丢失。

Conclusion: SHADOW成功解决了Kubernetes中有状态微服务迁移的停机问题，通过创新的并发运行和身份交换机制，在保证状态一致性和消息不丢失的前提下，实现了真正的零停机无缝迁移，显著提升了云原生环境中关键工作负载的可用性和灵活性。

Abstract: Migrating stateful microservices in Kubernetes requires careful state management because in-memory state is lost when a container restarts. For StatefulSet-managed workloads, the problem is compounded by identity constraints that prohibit two pods with the same ordinal from running simultaneously, forcing a sequential stop-recreate cycle with a median 38.5s of service downtime. This paper presents SHADOW Seamless Handoff And Zero-Downtime Orchestrated Workload Migration, a Kubernetes-native framework that implements the Message-based Stateful Microservice Migration (MS2M) approach as a Kubernetes Operator. SHADOW introduces the ShadowPod strategy, where a shadow pod is created from a CRIU checkpoint image on the target node while the source pod continues serving traffic, allowing concurrent operation during message replay. For StatefulSet workloads, an identity swap procedure with the ExchangeFence mechanism re-checkpoints the shadow pod, creates a StatefulSet-owned replacement, and drains both message queues to guarantee zero message loss during the handoff. An evaluation on a bare-metal Kubernetes cluster with 280 migration runs across four configurations and seven message rates (10--120msg/s) shows that, compared to the sequential baseline on the same StatefulSet workload, the ShadowPod strategy reduces the restore phase by up to 92%, eliminates service downtime entirely, and reduces total migration time by up to 77%, with zero message loss across all 280 runs.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Scalable Air-to-Ground Wireless Channel Modeling Using Environmental Context and Generative Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24620)
*Jingyi Tian,Lin Cai*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种环境感知的空地无线链路信道建模方法，利用真实环境数据与光线追踪技术，并训练扩散模型以实现高效、准确的信道性能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道（LEO）卫星的高速运动导致传播信道快速变化，现有研究多依赖假设的统计信道分布，忽视了实际地理环境的影响，尤其在低仰角时信号易受地形阻挡和环境影响。

Method: 结合数字高程模型（DEMs）和土地覆盖信息等真实环境数据，利用光线追踪（RT）判断链路的视距（LOS）或非视距（NLOS）状态并识别反射路径；结合衍射损耗、植被吸收和大气衰减模型定量表征信道；利用RT生成的样本和环境特征训练扩散模型，以高效预测任意位置的信道性能。

Result: 通过蜂窝网络和LEO卫星链路的实测数据验证，所提模型在真实环境中能够有效预测信道性能，支持实时决策。

Conclusion: 所提环境感知信道建模方法能够准确反映实际地理环境对信道的影响，并通过扩散模型实现了高效的实时信道预测，为LEO卫星通信系统提供了有效的建模工具。

Abstract: The fast motion of Low Earth Orbit (LEO) satellites causes the propagation channel to vary rapidly, and its behavior is strongly shaped by the surrounding environment, especially at low elevation angles where signals are highly susceptible to terrain blockage and other environmental effects. Existing studies mostly rely on assumed statistical channel distributions and therefore ignore the influence of the actual geographic environment. In this paper, we propose an environment-aware channel modeling method for air-to-ground wireless links. We leverage real environmental data, including digital elevation models (DEMs) and land cover information, together with ray tracing (RT) to determine whether a link is line-of-sight (LOS) or non-line-of-sight (NLOS) and to identify possible reflection paths of the signal. The resulting obstruction and reflection profiles are then combined with models of diffraction loss, vegetation absorption, and atmospheric attenuation to quantitatively characterize channel behavior in realistic geographic environments. Since RT is computationally intensive, we use RT-generated samples and environmental features to train a scalable diffusion model that can efficiently predict channel performance for arbitrary satellite and ground terminal positions, thereby supporting real-time decision-making. In the experiments, we validate the proposed model with measurement data from both cellular and LEO satellite links, demonstrating its effectiveness in realistic environments.

</details>


### [125] [Dual-Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Handover Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24634)
*Matteo Salvatori,Filippo Vannella,Sebastian Macaluso,Stylianos E. Trevlakis,Carlos Segura Perales,José Suarez-Varela,Alexandros-Apostolos A. Boulogeorgos,Ioannis Arapakis*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络的多智能体强化学习方法（TD3-D-MA），通过在对偶图上将小区个体偏移（CIO）优化建模为Dec-POMDP问题，实现了蜂窝网络切换参数的自适应优化，显著提升了网络吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蜂窝网络中的切换控制参数（特别是邻区对之间的CIO）配置依赖于基于规则的启发式方法，难以应对非平稳流量和移动性变化；且CIO调整在全网范围内具有紧密耦合性，微小的参数变化可能导致多邻居间的移动流重定向，静态规则在复杂场景下性能严重下降。

Method: 将切换优化问题在对偶图上建模为去中心化部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（Dec-POMDP），每个智能体控制一个邻区对的CIO并观测局部KPI；提出TD3-D-MA算法，采用共享参数的图神经网络（GNN）演员网络在对偶图上运行，结合区域级双重评论网络进行训练，以改善密集部署中的信用分配问题。

Result: 在配置真实运营商参数的ns-3系统级仿真器中，跨多种异构流量场景和网络拓扑进行评估；TD3-D-MA相比标准切换启发式方法和集中式RL基线显著提升了网络吞吐量，且在拓扑和流量变化时展现出良好的泛化能力和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于对偶图的Dec-POMDP建模结合GNN的多智能体强化学习方法能够有效解决蜂窝网络切换参数优化的可扩展性和耦合性问题，在真实网络环境下实现了优于传统方法和集中式方案的性能，具有良好的实际应用价值。

Abstract: HandOver (HO) control in cellular networks is governed by a set of HO control parameters that are traditionally configured through rule-based heuristics. A key parameter for HO optimization is the Cell Individual Offset (CIO), defined for each pair of neighboring cells and used to bias HO triggering decisions. At network scale, tuning CIOs becomes a tightly coupled problem: small changes can redirect mobility flows across multiple neighbors, and static rules often degrade under non-stationary traffic and mobility. We exploit the pairwise structure of CIOs by formulating HO optimization as a Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (Dec-POMDP) on the network's dual graph. In this representation, each agent controls a neighbor-pair CIO and observes Key Performance Indicators (KPIs) aggregated over its local dual-graph neighborhood, enabling scalable decentralized decisions while preserving graph locality. Building on this formulation, we propose TD3-D-MA, a discrete Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) variant of the TD3 algorithm with a shared-parameter Graph Neural Network (GNN) actor operating on the dual graph and region-wise double critics for training, improving credit assignment in dense deployments. We evaluate TD3-D-MA in an ns-3 system-level simulator configured with real-world network operator parameters across heterogeneous traffic regimes and network topologies. Results show that TD3-D-MA improves network throughput over standard HO heuristics and centralized RL baselines, and generalizes robustly under topology and traffic shifts.

</details>


### [126] [Spatio-Temporal Semantic Inference for Resilient 6G HRLLC in the Low-Altitude Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24712)
*Chuan-Chi Lai,Ang-Hsun Tsai,Zhu Han*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出EPIC框架，通过时空语义推断解耦协调环路与物理信令波动，实现93.5%的反应延迟降低并在极端信号静默期间提升10.5%的加权覆盖效率，为6G HRLLC提供确定性韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空经济（LAE）的快速扩张要求自主空中代理（AAA）之间具备高度可靠的协调能力。然而，6G网络中传统的反应式通信范式在复杂的城市峡谷环境中，日益受到随机网络抖动和间歇性信号静默的影响，导致连接性缺口，难以满足需求。

Method: 提出了具身主动推断协调（EPIC）框架，其核心是时空语义推断（STSI）算子。该算子通过将过时的对等观测投影到主动信念流形上，使协调环路解耦于物理信令波动，从而无论网络状态如何，都能维持确定性的反应延迟。

Result: 实验表明，EPIC实现了端到端反应延迟平均降低93.5%，将150毫秒的物理传输延迟掩盖为确定性的10毫秒执行心跳。关键在于，EPIC对高达100毫秒的网络抖动具有策略免疫力，并在长达50秒的极端信号静默期间，将加权覆盖效率（WCE）提高了10.5%。

Conclusion: 这些结果为6G超可靠低延迟通信（HRLLC）提供了必要的确定性韧性，证明了EPIC框架在应对复杂环境下的网络波动和保障自主空中代理协调可靠性方面的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Low-Altitude Economy (LAE) necessitates highly reliable coordination among autonomous aerial agents (AAAs). Traditional reactive communication paradigms in 6G networks are increasingly susceptible to stochastic network jitter and intermittent signaling silence, especially within complex urban canyon environments. To address this connectivity gap, this paper introduces the Embodied Proactive Inference for Coordination (EPIC) framework, featuring a Spatio-Temporal Semantic Inference (STSI) operator designed to decouple the coordination loop from physical signaling fluctuations. By projecting stale peer observations into a proactive belief manifold, EPIC maintains a deterministic reaction latency regardless of the network state. Extensive simulations demonstrate that EPIC achieves an average 93.5% reduction in end-to-end reaction latency, masking physical transmission delays of 150 ms with a deterministic 10 ms execution heartbeat. Crucially, EPIC exhibits strategic immunity to escalating network jitter up to 100 ms and improves the Weighted Coverage Efficiency (WCE) by 10.5% during extreme signaling silence lasting up to 50 s. These results provide the deterministic resilience essential for 6G Hyper-Reliable and Low-Latency Communication (HRLLC).

</details>


### [127] [Quantum Inspired Vehicular Network Optimization for Intelligent Decision Making in Smart Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24971)
*Kamran Ahmad Awan,Sonia Khan,Eman Abdullah Aldakheel,Saif Al-Kuwari,Ahmed Farouk*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了QIVNOM框架，一种受量子计算启发的优化方法，在经典边缘-云硬件上联合优化车联网通信与城市交通控制，相比最优基线将端到端延迟降低约20%，可靠性提升至96.7%，有效解决了网络中断和计算竞争下的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网联自动驾驶车辆需要满足严格的延迟和可靠性约束，而现有方法通常将通信优化与移动性优化分离处理，这会导致在网络中断和计算资源竞争情况下系统性能显著下降，因此迫切需要一种联合优化框架。

Method: 提出QIVNOM量子启发框架，将候选路由-信号方案编码为概率叠加态，利用球面投影梯度和退火采样更新方案；引入纠缠风格正则化项耦合网络与移动性决策，并结合Tchebycheff多目标标量化与可行性投影以强制执行延迟和可靠性约束。

Result: 在METR-LA校准的SUMO-OMNeT++/Veins仿真中，QIVNOM将平均端到端延迟降至57.3ms（比最优基线低约20%）；在事故场景下延迟从79ms降至62ms（-21.5%），在RSU中断场景下从86ms降至67ms（-22.1%）；数据包投递率达96.7%，可靠性维持在96.7%；在走廊封闭场景下平均出行时间降至12.8分钟，拥堵率降至33%。

Conclusion: QIVNOM证明了在经典硬件上采用量子启发方法联合优化V2V/V2I通信与交通控制的可行性，在正常运行、事故及基础设施故障等多种场景下均实现了显著的延迟降低与可靠性提升，为城市规模的网联自动驾驶车辆协调提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Connected and automated vehicles require city-scale coordination under strict latency and reliability constraints. However, many existing approaches optimize communication and mobility separately, which can degrade performance during network outages and under compute contention. This paper presents QIVNOM, a quantum-inspired framework that jointly optimizes vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) and vehicle-to-infrastructure (V2I) communication together with urban traffic control on classical edge--cloud hardware, without requiring a quantum processor. QIVNOM encodes candidate routing--signal plans as probabilistic superpositions and updates them using sphere-projected gradients with annealed sampling to minimize a regularized objective. An entanglement-style regularizer couples networking and mobility decisions, while Tchebycheff multi-objective scalarization with feasibility projection enforces constraints on latency and reliability.
  The proposed framework is evaluated in METR-LA--calibrated SUMO--OMNeT++/Veins simulations over a $5\times5$~km urban map with IEEE 802.11p and 5G NR sidelink. Results show that QIVNOM reduces mean end-to-end latency to 57.3~ms, approximately $20\%$ lower than the best baseline. Under incident conditions, latency decreases from 79~ms to 62~ms ($-21.5\%$), while under roadside unit (RSU) outages, it decreases from 86~ms to 67~ms ($-22.1\%$). Packet delivery reaches $96.7\%$ (an improvement of $+2.3$ percentage points), and reliability remains $96.7\%$ overall, including $96.8\%$ under RSU outages versus $94.1\%$ for the baseline. In corridor-closure scenarios, travel performance also improves, with average travel time reduced to 12.8~min and congestion lowered to $33\%$, compared with 14.5~min and $37\%$ for the baseline.

</details>


### [128] [A Wireless World Model for AI-Native 6G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25216)
*Ziqi Chen,Yi Ren,Yixuan Huang,Qi Sun,Nan Li,Yuhong Huang,Chih-Lin I,Yifan Li,Liang Xia*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了无线世界模型（WWM），这是一个多模态基础框架，通过内化3D几何与信号动力学之间的因果关系来预测无线信道的时空演化。该模型在大规模射线追踪多模态数据集上进行预训练，在五种关键下游任务中均优于现有的单模态基础模型和任务特定模型，为物理感知的6G智能奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人工智能集成到物理层是6G网络的基石，然而当前的数据驱动方法缺乏对电磁波传播的内在理解，难以在动态环境中进行泛化。现有方法存在数据真实性差距，无法有效适应复杂多变的无线传播环境。

Method: 提出无线世界模型（WWM），采用联合嵌入预测架构和多模态专家混合Transformer，将信道状态信息、3D点云和用户轨迹融合为统一表示。模型在大规模射线追踪多模态数据集上进行预训练，内化了3D几何与信号动力学之间的因果关系。

Result: WWM在已见环境、未见泛化场景和真实世界测量中均取得显著性能，在五种关键下游任务中持续优于最先进的单模态基础模型和任务特定模型，并通过真实世界测量数据进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: WWM成功克服了数据真实性差距，实现了对物理世界的适应性，为物理感知的6G智能开辟了道路，展示了将物理原理融入AI模型的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Integrating AI into the physical layer is a cornerstone of 6G networks. However, current data-driven approaches struggle to generalize across dynamic environments because they lack an intrinsic understanding of electromagnetic wave propagation. We introduce the Wireless World Model (WWM), a multi-modal foundation framework predicting the spatiotemporal evolution of wireless channels by internalizing the causal relationship between 3D geometry and signal dynamics. Pre-trained on a massive ray-traced multi-modal dataset, WWM overcomes the data authenticity gap, further validated under real-world measurement data. Using a joint-embedding predictive architecture with a multi-modal mixture-of-experts Transformer, WWM fuses channel state information, 3D point clouds, and user trajectories into a unified representation. Across the five key downstream tasks supported by WWM, it achieves remarkable performance in seen environments, unseen generalization scenarios, and real-world measurements, consistently outperforming SOTA uni-modal foundation models and task-specific models. This paves the way for physics-aware 6G intelligence that adapts to the physical world.

</details>


### [129] [Lightweight GenAI for Network Traffic Synthesis: Fidelity, Augmentation, and Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25507)
*Giampaolo Bovenzi,Domenico Ciuonzo,Jonatan Krolikowski,Antonio Montieri,Alfredo Nascita,Antonio Pescapè,Dario Rossi*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出利用轻量级生成式AI架构（包括Transformer、状态空间和扩散模型）解决网络流量生成任务，在有限标签数据和隐私约束下实现高质量流量合成。实验表明Transformer模型在保真度和效率之间取得最佳平衡，分类器在纯合成数据训练下达到87% F1值，数据增强在低数据场景下提升性能高达40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的网络流量分类日益受到有限标签数据和严格隐私要求的限制。虽然网络流量生成可以有效缓解数据稀缺问题，但传统生成方法难以建模现代流量的复杂时序动态特性，且通常产生显著的计算成本。

Method: 采用轻量级生成式AI架构进行网络流量生成，包括基于Transformer的模型、状态空间模型和扩散模型。从四个维度进行系统评估：(i)合成流量保真度，(ii)纯合成数据训练，(iii)低数据环境下的数据增强，(iv)计算效率。在两个异构数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 轻量级GenAI模型能够保持流量的静态和时序特征，Transformer和状态空间模型在所有保真度指标上紧密匹配真实分布。纯合成流量训练的分类器在真实数据上达到高达87% F1值。在低数据设置下，GenAI驱动的数据增强将NTC性能提升高达+40%，显著缩小与全数据训练的差距。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的模型在保真度和效率之间提供了最佳权衡，能够以适度的计算开销实现高质量、隐私感知的流量合成，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate Network Traffic Classification (NTC) is increasingly constrained by limited labeled data and strict privacy requirements. While Network Traffic Generation (NTG) provides an effective means to mitigate data scarcity, conventional generative methods struggle to model the complex temporal dynamics of modern traffic or/and often incur significant computational cost. In this article, we address the NTG task using lightweight Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) architectures, including transformer-based, state-space, and diffusion models designed for practical deployment.
