We conduct a systematic evaluation along four axes: (i) (synthetic) traffic fidelity, (ii) synthetic-only training, (iii) data augmentation under low-data regimes, and (iv) computational efficiency. Experiments on two heterogeneous datasets show that lightweight GenAI models preserve both static and temporal traffic characteristics, with transformer and state-space models closely matching real distributions across a complete set of fidelity metrics. Classifiers trained solely on synthetic traffic achieve up to 87% F1-score on real data. In low-data settings, GenAI-driven augmentation improves NTC performance by up to +40%, substantially reducing the gap with full-data training. Overall, transformer-based models provide the best trade-off between fidelity and efficiency, enabling high-quality, privacy-aware traffic synthesis with modest computational overhead.

</details>


### [130] [Cooperative Deep Reinforcement Learning for Fair RIS Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25572)
*Martin Mark Zan,Stefan Schwarz*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于公平感知协作多智能体强化学习的RIS资源分配框架，通过拍卖机制在多基站间动态分配RIS，有效提升了边缘用户速率并平衡了网络效率与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多小区无线网络中部署可重构智能表面（RIS）带来了资源分配的新挑战，尤其是当各基站间的用户负载不均衡时。RIS作为共享基础设施需要在竞争的基站间动态分配，传统方法难以兼顾系统整体效率与各小区间的公平性。

Method: 采用同时升价拍卖机制将RIS作为共享基础设施动态分配给竞争的基站。提出公平感知的协作多智能体强化学习方法，基站根据预期效用增益和相对服务质量调整竞价策略。引入中心化计算的性能相关公平性指标作为智能体观测的一部分，实现基站间无需直接通信的隐式协调。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该框架能有效将RIS资源重新分配给性能较差的小区，显著提升了最差服务用户的速率，同时保持了整体吞吐量。证明了通过协作学习可以实现面向公平的RIS分配。

Conclusion: 基于公平导向的RIS分配可以通过协作学习实现，为未来无线网络中平衡效率与公平性提供了灵活的工具。该方法在保证系统整体性能的同时，有效改善了网络公平性。

Abstract: The deployment of reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RISs) introduces new challenges for resource allocation in multi-cell wireless networks, particularly when user loads are uneven across base stations. In this work, we consider RISs as shared infrastructure that must be dynamically assigned among competing base stations, and we address this problem using a simultaneous ascending auction mechanism. To mitigate performance imbalances between cells, we propose a fairness-aware collaborative multi-agent reinforcement learning approach in which base stations adapt their bidding strategies based on both expected utility gains and relative service quality. A centrally computed performance-dependent fairness indicator is incorporated into the agents' observations, enabling implicit coordination without direct inter-base-station communication. Simulation results show that the proposed framework effectively redistributes RIS resources toward weaker-performing cells, substantially improving the rates of the worst-served users while preserving overall throughput. The results demonstrate that fairness-oriented RIS allocation can be achieved through cooperative learning, providing a flexible tool for balancing efficiency and equity in future wireless networks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Causal AI For AMS Circuit Design: Interpretable Parameter Effects Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24618)
*Mohyeu Hussain,David Koblah,Reiner Dizon-Paradis,Domenic Forte*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于因果推理的框架，通过从SPICE数据中发现有向无环图(DAG)并估计平均处理效应(ATE)，实现了对模拟混合信号电路设计参数的可解释性排序和预测，准确度显著优于神经网络方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟混合信号(AMS)电路具有高度非线性特性且处理连续信号，相比数字电路更难用数据驱动的AI方法进行建模。现有方法难以弥合结构化设计数据(器件尺寸、偏置电压等)与实际性能之间的差距，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 提出一个因果推理框架：首先从SPICE仿真数据中发现有向无环图(DAG)结构，然后通过平均处理效应(ATE)估计来量化参数影响。该方法能够产生人类可解释的设计旋钮排序，并提供明确的'假设分析'预测能力。

Result: 在TSMC 65nm工艺的三种运算放大器系列(OTA、望远镜式、折叠共源共栅)上验证，因果模型重现基于仿真的ATE时平均绝对误差小于25%，而神经网络基准方法偏差超过80%且经常预测错误的符号。

Conclusion: 因果AI方法在AMS电路设计中同时提供了更高的准确性和可解释性，能够帮助设计者理解尺寸和拓扑结构之间的权衡关系，为更高效、可信赖的AMS设计自动化铺平了道路。

Abstract: Analog-mixed-signal (AMS) circuits are highly non-linear and operate on continuous real-world signals, making them far more difficult to model with data-driven AI than digital blocks. To close the gap between structured design data (device dimensions, bias voltages, etc.) and real-world performance, we propose a causal-inference framework that first discovers a directed-acyclic graph (DAG) from SPICE simulation data and then quantifies parameter impact through Average Treatment Effect (ATE) estimation. The approach yields human-interpretable rankings of design knobs and explicit 'what-if' predictions, enabling designers to understand trade-offs in sizing and topology. We evaluate the pipeline on three operational-amplifier families (OTA, telescopic, and folded-cascode) implemented in TSMC 65nm and benchmark it against a baseline neural-network (NN) regressor. Across all circuits the causal model reproduces simulation-based ATEs with an average absolute error of less than 25%, whereas the neural network deviates by more than 80% and frequently predicts the wrong sign. These results demonstrate that causal AI provides both higher accuracy and explainability, paving the way for more efficient, trustworthy AMS design automation.

</details>


### [132] [TAMI-MPC:Trusted Acceleration of Minimal-Interaction MPC for Efficient Nonlinear Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24861)
*Zhuoran Li,Hanieh Totonchi Asl,Yifei Cai,Ebrahim Nouri,Danella Zhao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了TAMI-MPC框架，利用可信执行环境（TEE）加速安全多方计算中的非线性运算，通过优化核心原语和消除不经意传输（OT），在ResNet-50和BERT-base推理上分别实现了最高4.86倍和7.44倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全多方计算（MPC）系统依赖通信和计算密集型原语（如非线性推理中的安全比较），在资源受限平台上难以实现实时推理，亟需高效的解决方案。

Method: 1. 重新设计核心原语（叶比较和树合并），将交互轮数从log(n)降至每次操作仅需1轮；2. 利用TEE内部同步种子消除大部分设计中的不经意传输（OT），并结合相关随机数复用技术降低计算开销；3. 设计专用加速器，重构各阶段数据流以实现连续、细粒度流处理和高并行性，降低内存开销。

Result: 在ResNet-50推理任务上相比最先进CNN框架实现最高4.86倍加速，在BERT-base推理任务上相比最先进LLM框架实现最高7.44倍加速。

Conclusion: TAMI-MPC框架有效解决了资源受限平台上MPC非线性评估的通信和计算瓶颈，显著提升了隐私保护机器学习的实时推理性能。

Abstract: Secure multi-party computation (MPC) offers a practical foundation for privacy-preserving machine learning at the edge. However, current MPC systems rely heavily on communication and computation-intensive primitives-such as secure comparison for nonlinear inference, which are often impractical on resource-constrained platforms. To enable real-time inference under a resource-constrained platform, we introduce a Trusted Acceleration of Minimal-Interaction MPC framework, TAMI-MPC, for nonlinear evaluation. Specifically, we reduce communication cost by redesigning the core primitives, leaf comparison, and tree merge, reducing the interactive round from log(n) to just 1 per operation. Furthermore, unlike prior work that heavily relies on oblivious transfer (OT), a well-known computational bottleneck, we leverage synchronized seeds inside the TEE to eliminate OT for the vast majority of our designs, along with a correlated-randomness reuse technique that keeps new designs computationally lightweight. To fully realize the potential, we design a specialized accelerator that restructures the dataflow across stages to enable continuous, fine-grained streaming and high parallelism, reducing memory overhead. Our design achieves up to 4.86x speedup on ResNet-50 inference, compared with state-of-the-art CNN frameworks, and achieves up to 7.44x speedup on BERT-base inference, compared with state-of-the-art LLM frameworks.

</details>


### [133] [Surrogates, Spikes, and Sparsity: Performance Analysis and Characterization of SNN Hyperparameters on Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.24891)
*Ilkin Aliyev,Jesus Lopez,Tosiron Adegbija*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了SNN中训练超参数（代理梯度函数和神经元模型）对硬件推理延迟的关键影响。研究表明，标准精度指标无法有效预测硬件效率；通过合理选择Spike Rate Escape函数和Lapicque神经元模型，可在精度损失极小的情况下实现高达28%的延迟降低，在FPGA平台上验证了超过2倍的延迟改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲神经网络（SNN）理论上具有低功耗推理优势，但由于训练时选择与推理时稀疏性之间的关系不透明，理论能效优势往往与实际硬件性能脱节。现有研究主要关注权重剪枝和压缩，而训练超参数（特别是代理梯度函数和神经元模型配置）对硬件级激活稀疏性的影响尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 提出一项工作负载特征化研究，量化硬件延迟对SNN超参数的敏感性。研究解耦了代理梯度函数（如Fast Sigmoid、Spike Rate Escape）和神经元模型（LIF、Lapicque）对分类精度和推理效率的影响。在三个基于事件的视觉数据集（DVS128-Gesture、N-MNIST和DVS-CIFAR10）上进行实验，并在定制的周期精确FPGA-based SNN仪器平台上进行验证。

Result: 实验发现标准精度指标是硬件效率的糟糕预测因子。虽然Fast Sigmoid在DVS-CIFAR10上达到最高精度，但Spike Rate Escape在DVS128-Gesture上将推理延迟降低了12.2%，且精度损失极小。神经元模型选择与参数调优同样关键：从LIF切换到Lapicque神经元可实现高达28%的延迟降低。稀疏性感知的超参数选择可将精度提高9.1%，延迟改善超过2倍。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了一套从训练参数预测硬件行为的方法论，证明了稀疏性感知的超参数选择对SNN硬件部署的重要性。研究发现神经元模型选择与参数调优同等重要，为SNN的硬件感知训练提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) offer inherent advantages for low-power inference through sparse, event-driven computation. However, the theoretical energy benefits of SNNs are often decoupled from real hardware performance due to the opaque relationship between training-time choices and inference-time sparsity. While prior work has focused on weight pruning and compression, the role of training hyperparameters -- specifically surrogate gradient functions and neuron model configurations -- in shaping hardware-level activation sparsity remains underexplored.
  This paper presents a workload characterization study quantifying the sensitivity of hardware latency to SNN hyperparameters. We decouple the impact of surrogate gradient functions (e.g., Fast Sigmoid, Spike Rate Escape) and neuron models (LIF, Lapicque) on classification accuracy and inference efficiency across three event-based vision datasets: DVS128-Gesture, N-MNIST, and DVS-CIFAR10. Our analysis reveals that standard accuracy metrics are poor predictors of hardware efficiency. While Fast Sigmoid achieves the highest accuracy on DVS-CIFAR10, Spike Rate Escape reduces inference latency by up to 12.2% on DVS128-Gesture with minimal accuracy trade-offs. We also demonstrate that neuron model selection is as critical as parameter tuning; transitioning from LIF to Lapicque neurons yields up to 28% latency reduction. We validate on a custom cycle-accurate FPGA-based SNN instrumentation platform, showing that sparsity-aware hyperparameter selection can improve accuracy by 9.1% and latency by over 2x compared to baselines. These findings establish a methodology for predicting hardware behavior from training parameters. The RTL and reproducibility artifacts are at https://zenodo.org/records/18893738.

</details>


### [134] [AutoPDR: Circuit-Aware Solver Configuration Prediction for Hardware Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25048)
*Guangyu Hu,Chen Chen,Xiaofeng Zhou,Jiaxi Zhang,Wei Zhang,Hongce Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于图学习的电路感知求解器配置框架，用于PDR形式化验证的智能启发式选择。通过结合图表示和静态电路特征预测最优配置，并利用专家先验知识进行约束过滤减少78%搜索空间，实验证明相比默认配置和常用设置有显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDR算法在硬件和软件系统形式化验证中性能高度依赖参数配置。人工参数调优耗时且需要领域专业知识，而传统的自动化参数调优框架不适合PDR这类时间敏感的验证任务。

Method: 提出电路感知求解器配置框架：1) 结合图表示与静态电路特征预测特定电路的最优PDR求解配置；2) 通过基于约束的参数过滤融入专家先验知识，消除无效和低效配置；3) 特征提取流程捕获电路拓扑和组件模式的结构、功能和连接特性。

Result: 1) 搜索空间减少78%；2) 在综合基准测试套件上相比默认配置和常用设置获得显著性能提升；3) 成功识别电路特定参数模式并自动选择最适合的求解策略。

Conclusion: 该系统能够基于电路特性自动选择最适合的求解策略，是自动化形式化验证工作流的实用工具，有效解决了PDR参数调优的关键挑战。

Abstract: Property Directed Reachability (PDR) is a powerful algorithm for formal verification of hardware and software systems, but its performance is highly sensitive to parameter configurations. Manual parameter tuning is time-consuming and requires domain expertise, while traditional automated parameter tuning frameworks are not well-suited for time-sensitive verification tasks like PDR. This paper presents a circuit-aware solver configuration framework that employs graph learning for intelligent heuristic selection in PDR-based verification. Our approach combines graph representations with static circuit features to predict optimal PDR solving configurations for specific circuits. We incorporate expert prior knowledge through constraint-based parameter filtering to eliminate invalid and inefficient configurations and reduce 78% search space. Our feature extraction pipeline captures structural, functional, and connectivity characteristics of circuit topology and component patterns. Experimental evaluation on a comprehensive benchmark suite demonstrates significant performance improvements compared to default configurations and commonly-used settings. The system successfully identifies circuit-specific parameter patterns and automatically selects the most suitable solving strategies based on circuit characteristics, making it a practical tool for automated formal verification workflows.

</details>


### [135] [FluxEDA: A Unified Execution Infrastructure for Stateful Agentic EDA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.25243)
*Zhengrui Chen,Zixuan Song,Yu Li,Qi Sun,Cheng Zhuo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了FluxEDA，一个面向智能体EDA的统一有状态基础设施，通过网关执行接口和持久化后端实例解决了现有集成方案难以保持工具状态和迭代优化的问题，在商业后端案例中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM与智能体EDA集成方案多依赖脚本级或请求级交互，难以在生产环境中保持工具状态并支持迭代优化。

Method: 提出FluxEDA框架，引入基于受管网关的执行接口以处理结构化请求与响应，并维护持久化的后端实例，使上层智能体可通过保留的运行时状态与异构EDA工具交互，而非通过隔离的Shell调用。

Result: 在两个代表性商业后端案例（自动布线后时序ECO和标准单元子库优化）上的评估表明，FluxEDA能支持真实工具环境下的多步分析与优化，包括状态复用、回滚及协调迭代执行。

Conclusion: 有状态且受控的基础设施层是构建智能体辅助EDA自动化的实用基础。

Abstract: Large language models and autonomous agents are increasingly explored for EDA automation, but many existing integrations still rely on script-level or request-level interactions, which makes it difficult to preserve tool state and support iterative optimization in real production-oriented environments. In this work, we present FluxEDA, a unified and stateful infrastructure substrate for agentic EDA. FluxEDA introduces a managed gateway-based execution interface with structured request and response handling. It also maintains persistent backend instances. Together, these features allow upper-layer agents and programmable clients to interact with heterogeneous EDA tools through preserved runtime state, rather than through isolated shell invocations. We evaluate the framework using two representative commercial backend case studies: automated post-route timing ECO and standard-cell sub-library optimization. The results show that FluxEDA can support multi-step analysis and optimization over real tool contexts, including state reuse, rollback, and coordinated iterative execution. These findings suggest that a stateful and governed infrastructure layer is a practical foundation for agent-assisted EDA automation.

</details>
